My fellow Journeyman, we face grave times.\nHopefully our Master will find a way for us to leave this troubled region and return safely to Alvar.\nI would not wish to end my life here, even though it is the place of my birth.	同志们，我们现在遇到的是非常危险的状况。很幸运的，我们的主人会找到方法让我们离开这个要命的地方，安全的回到阿尔瓦。虽然这是我出生的地方，但我可不想要在这边把命给送掉。
Dadeross led us here to trade with the Lizardman Clan of Onefang for their tobersk fruit.\nHe has gone to talk with Brekish Onefang, the village leader.\nPerhaps you should look for him?	达德洛斯带领我们来到这边，准备让我们和蜥蜴人的部落首领单牙交易他们的托伯斯克果。他刚去找布拉克许·单牙谈话。也许你们可以去找他？
The Regnan Pirates are a threat to the economy and free travel in Jadame.\nVery few boats ply the sea, for fear of being sunk…or worse!\nWhen in Ravenshore, I did hear rumors of a small band of smugglers who have been working beneath the notice of the pirates.\nI wonder how they do that!	雷格那的海盗是贾丹姆大陆上贸易和四处旅行的最大威胁。海上只有极少的船只愿意四处旅行，因为担心被这些恐怖的家伙给弄沉……或是遇到更糟糕的命运！当你在渡鸦海滨的时候，我听说过有一小群走私者在海盗的肆虐之下依旧照旧工作。不知道他们是怎么办到的！
It is as I have read in the prophecy of the Unmaking! "...and a Mountain of Fire shall rise from the sea, like a wound drawn by the quick stroke of a dagger…".You must find a way off of these islands, if not for the caravan, at least for yourself!\nSomeone must take news of what has happened here to the Merchant Council in Alvar!\nThey must know of the cataclysm and of the Regnan raid!	这就像是我在末日预言中读过的一样！“……火之大山将会从海中升起，就像是匕首一划所造成的伤口一样……”\n 你一定要找到离开这些岛屿的方法，即使不为了车队，也为了你自己！一定要有人离开这里，把这边的消息告诉阿尔瓦商团公会的议会！他们必须要知道大灾变的状况和雷格那海盗的入侵才行！
The pirates from the dread Island of Regna are using the cataclysm and the confusion created by it to aid them in overrunning the islands.\nIf they conquer Dagger Wound, they can use it as an outpost for direct raids on Ravenshore!\nThe Merchant Council in Alvar must be notified!\nYou must find a way to the boats and tell them!	从恐怖的岛屿雷格那来的海盗正在利用这场大灾变和它所造成的混乱四处劫掠。如果他们征服了匕伤群岛，他们就可以利用这个地方当做前哨站，攻击渡鸦海滨！阿尔瓦议会的成员必须要知道这件事情才行！你必须要找到可以离开此处的船，并且告诉他们这个消息！
Good work dealing with those pirates. Now that they're gone, maybe Chief Onefang and his people can rebuild their lives. It really is a travesty what has happened here.	多谢你们处理掉那些海盗。在那些海盗被赶走之后，也许单牙酋长和他的同胞们可以重建村庄。在这里发生过的事情实在是太糟糕了。
It will take several boats to ferry the entire caravan back to Ravenshore, or many trips by one boat.\nThis cannot be done until the waters around the Dagger Wound Islands are free from Regnan Pirates.\nIt is best that you return to Alvar and tell the Council of the Regnan attack.\nThey will send help to rescue us!	要把车队整个运回渡鸦海滨去，我们必须要好几艘船，或者是一艘船来回好几趟才行。在雷格那海盗离开匕伤群岛附近的海面之前，我们没有办法办到这件事情。你最好赶快回到阿尔瓦去，并且告诉议会雷格那海盗攻击的这件事情。他们将会派出援军来拯救我们。
Sadly, the caravan was one of the first things the pirates looted in their raids. I have no idea what they're going to do with three-hundred cases of tobersk fruit! Naturally, I miss the fruit, but the worst of it is the damage they did to the carts. It looks like I'll be here for at least the next season overseeing their repair. I just hope the task is done before the next tobersk harvest so I can see some return out of this profitless venture.	很遗憾的，海盗们一来到这边就开始抢劫这车队。我实在想不出来他们到底有三百箱的托伯斯克果干嘛！当然，我觉得水果被抢走了很可惜，但是他们对这些车子所造成的损害更让人痛心。看起来我至少要在这边待到下一季才能够修好这些车子。我只希望这整个整修工程能够在下一次托伯斯克果采收之前完成，好让我勉强捞回一笔来。
In the middle of the night the ground shook!\nFlaming rocks dropped from the sky!\nMany of my people were hurt or killed!\nGreat damage was done to the village.\nThe bridges that allowed travel between the smaller islands and the main island were destroyed.\nWe found ourselves stuck on the main island, unable to reach those on the smaller islands who may be injured.\nMany family members and friends are missing.\nWe cannot reach the islands with the boat docks, and thus we are unable to send to Ravenshore for help. I do not even know if any boats survived!	在半夜的时候，整个大地突然开始震动起来！着火的岩石从天上降落！我的许多同胞都因此而受伤或者是被杀死了！村庄里面受到了很大的伤害。在各岛屿和大陆之间所搭建的桥梁也都跟着损坏了。我们发现自己被困在这座岛屿上，无法拯救那些被困在其它岛屿上的同胞。许多家人和朋友都在这场灾害中失踪了。我们无法利用船只前往其它岛屿，因此我们也没有办法前往渡鸦海滨求救。我甚至不知道有没有船只在这场灾难中留存下来。
What with the damage caused by giant waves, falling rocks and the pirate raid, I don't know if our village will ever be the same. I know you have done great things for us, but not everything can be fixed once broken. I'm not sure what we'll do. Perhaps we will rebuild, or maybe we'll leave. Time and circumstance will decide.	再经历过了巨浪、落石和海盗的劫掠之后，我不知道我们的村庄会不会再像以前一样。我知道你已经替我们做了很多，但并不是每样东西都可以恢复旧观。也许我们会重建，也许我们必须离开。也许时间到了我们就会知道。
In time long past, my people used the Portals of Stone to travel quickly from island to island, but we have lost the knowledge of their operation. Only the pair connecting the village to the southwestern fields still functions. Now that the bridges are gone, we're trapped on the island!	在很久以前，我们的同胞会利用岩石传送门在岛与岛之间快速的往来，但是我们已经遗忘了要怎么操作这些东西。只有一座连接这个村庄和西南岛的传送门还依旧可以运作。现在桥梁都已经毁坏了，我们等于被困在这座岛屿上了！
A Cleric, by the name of Fredrick Talimere, has been living with us for the last few years.\nHe has been studying the Portals of Stone and the outer ruins of the Abandoned Temple.\nMaybe he has the knowledge to get the Portals working again!\nFind him and see if he will help!	一名叫做费德瑞克·塔力米尔的牧师曾经和我们居住过一阵子。他曾经在废弃神庙那边研究岩石传送门。也许他知道怎么让这些传送门再度启动！去找他，看看他愿不愿意帮忙！
Ah yes…"the portals." I have been studying them and the lost culture that built them for years.They were built by the civilization that had its day long before the Lizardmen came to inhabit these islands. They were a means of instantaneous travel between the islands. Sadly, the power stones needed to operate them are in short supply.	啊，是的……传送门。我研究它们和建造它们的神秘文化好多年了。\n 他们是在蜥蜴人来到这里之前许多旧有的文明之下所建造的。他们是用来在不同的岛屿之间传送用的工具。很遗憾的，能够启动这些传送门的能源之石目前数量非常的稀少。
The first night of the mountain of fire, I went to the beach to see if I could see anything of its eruption.\nI cannot be certain, but as the volcano continued to erupt, I thought I saw Earth Elementals roaming the lava encrusted base of the mountain.	当着火山脉降落的第一天时，我到海滩那边去观看，想要知道是否能够看到任何有关它爆发的情形。其实我不太确定，但是我想我从山脉的底端看到了有许多土元素争先恐后地冒出来。
So Clanleader Onefang gave you that power stone he was holding onto! It will power the portal on the southwestern tip of the island. To use it, hold an image of the stone in your mind as you step onto the portal.	啊，单牙酋长把他所拥有的能源之石给了你！这将可以启动岛上西南方的传送门。你只需要把这块石头的影像铭记在脑海中，然后就可以启动传送门。
The ruins of a temple built by the lost civilization lies on the island just northwest of the main island. I have not explored it however, for dangerous reptiles have made it their lair. I do suspect that the temple leads to an underwater tunnel which surfaces near the boat docks on the northwest island.With the bridges out, that tunnel may be the only way to reach the docks and a boat to safety!	建造传送门的失落文明所盖的神庙废墟就在主岛屿西北方的地方。不过，因为里面有很多危险的爬虫类，所以其实我并没有探索过里面到底有什么东西。不过，我倒是怀疑庙里面可能有个水下隧道，通往西北方岛上的那座港口。\n 在桥梁都被摧毁之后，那座隧道也许成为了唯一抵达港口或是船只的路径！
My brother, Isthric the Tongue, went to check on the tobersk plants on one of the lesser islands.\nHe has not returned!\nI am afraid that he is one of those stranded by the cataclysm. He may even be hurt!\nIf you were to fix the Portals of Stone, he would surely be able to return, and we could get help to those who need it!\nFind him for me!	我的弟兄利舌埃斯瑞克去其它的小岛上检查托伯斯克水果的生长状况。但是他没有回来！我很担心他可能被大灾变困在其它的岛上了。他甚至可能受伤了！如果你能够修复好岩石传送门，他应该可以回来，我们也可以替那些需要帮助的人找到足够的协助！赶快替我找到他！
You found Isthric and told him how to return home?\nWe are indeed in debt to you, Merchant of Alvar!\nI will speak well of you to Clan Leader Brekish Onefang.\nPlease take these potions of Cure Wounds as a reward.	你找到了埃斯瑞克了，并且告诉他要回家了吗？我们真的非常感激你！我会告诉我们的酋长布瑞克许·单牙。请把这些疗伤药剂收下，当作我对你的回报。
My Granda used to tell us of the times past, when the Portals of Stone were the main travel route between the islands.\nShe told us that the keys to the Portals disappeared during one of the first raids committed by the Regnan Pirates.\nIf the Portals of Stone were working my brother Isthric could return back to the main island!	我的祖母曾经告诉过我们，很久以前，岩石传送门是各岛屿之间旅行的主要工具。她告诉我们，在第一次雷格那海盗侵略这座岛屿的时候，岩石传送门的钥匙也跟着消失了。如果岩石传送门能够恢复运作，那么我的兄弟利舌埃斯瑞克也可以回到这座岛上了！
You have repaired the Portals of Stone?\nThat is tremendous news!\nQuickly, find my brother, Isthric and tell him to return home!	你已经修好了岩石传送门？太好了！赶快叫我的兄弟埃斯瑞克回家来！
That rude man?\nHe's more concerned with that Abandoned Temple than he is with the life that goes on around him!\nMy Granda warned him that the Temple is a place of great Evil.\nHe never listened.\nNow Granda isn't with us anymore and we have no one but Brekish Onefang to remind us of the past.	那个粗鲁的家伙？比起自己的生活，他更关心的是那座废弃神庙！我祖母说过，那座废弃神庙是个十分邪恶的地方，他根本不听。现在祖母已经走了。除了布拉克许·单牙以外已经没人能提醒我们警戒过去的教训了。\nNow Granda isn't with us anymore and we have no one but Brekish Onefang to remind us of the past.
We have offended the Ancients!\nWhy else would they call up the Mountain of Fire!\nThe Prophecies tell of a time when the oceans would boil!\nThis is the beginning of the end!	我们一定冒犯了古人！不然他们为什么会召唤来着火的大山！预言曾经说过有一天大海将会沸腾！这就是末日的开始！\nThis is the beginning of the end!
There are many Prophecies that tell of the destruction of Jadame and the entire world!\n Most tell of a time when the world will be consumed by that which created it.\nPerhaps the Ancients have decided to wipe us all out!	有许多预言里面提到了贾丹姆大陆和整个世界的毁灭。这许多记载中，提到了整个世界将会被创造它的力量所摧毁。也许古人们决定要将我们给彻底摧毁掉！\nPerhaps the Ancients have decided to wipe us all out!
There is one Prophecy, the Prophecy of the Snake, that I have been unable to find a copy of.\nI think it may be most revealing about the future of Jadame.Fredrick Talimere, the Cleric, has told me of the snake ruins, and of the Abandoned Temple.\nHe is in agreement with me, that there may be a copy of this prophecy, somewhere in the temple.\nCould you find it for me?	有一个预言，叫做蛇之预言，我一直找不到它的任何抄本。我觉得那个预言也许预示了贾丹姆大陆的未来。\n 牧师费德瑞克·塔力米尔曾经跟我提到过这些蛇人的废墟，以及一个废弃神庙。他同意我的看法：也许在这些建筑物中会有这个预言的抄本。你能够替我找到它吗？
You have found the Prophecies of the Snake!\nPerhaps the details of our future can be found in its writings!\nPlease take this reward for your assistance!	你找到了蛇之预言！也许我们的未来就记载在其中！请收下这些东西当作我对你的回报！\nPlease take this reward for your assistance!
You must find a way off of these islands. Someone must take news of what has happened here to our masters, the Merchants of Alvar!\nThey must know of the cataclysm and of the Regnan raid! Take this to him now.	你必须要找到一个离开这些岛屿的方法。一定要有人把这里发生的事情通知我们的上级，阿尔瓦的商人们！他们一定要知道这里发生的大灾变和雷格那海盗的所作所为！\n 我已经写好了一封信，你必须要把它交给渡鸦海滨的阿尔瓦商人代表，艾尔加·费尔蒙。如果这世界上有任何人有资格建议我们该作些什么，费尔蒙一定是其中之一。赶快把这封信交给他。
You must bring my letter to Elgar Fellmoon in Ravenshore!	你一定要将这封信带给在渡鸦海滨的艾德加·费尔蒙！
Take this crystal to Fredrick Talimere. I know that it has something to do with the portals of stone. Perhaps he can tell you how it functions.	把这个水晶带去给费德瑞克·塔力米尔。我知道这和岩石传送门一定有些关系。也许他可以告诉你这个石头和那些传送门有什么关系！
Bring the portal crystal to Fredrick Talimere. He will know what it does.	把这个传送门水晶交给费德瑞克·塔力米尔。他会知道这有什么用的。
The Merchant Council had sent our greatest warrior, Cauri Blackthorne, to consult with the Sun Temple priests about the disasters that have struck all of Jadame.Cauri left for the Temple of the Sun in Murmurwoods shortly after rumors of the cataclysms were heard here in Alvar.\nShe was to return after consulting the priests.\nWe are very concerned that she has not returned.As for your promotion, only Cauri can accurately test your skills and assess your worthiness for promotion.\nIf you find her, provide her with any assistance she may need and return here with news of her status and location.	商团议会已经派出了我们最强悍的战士，考瑞·黑王座来和太阳神庙讨论即将降临贾丹姆大陆的大灾难。\n 考瑞在来到阿尔瓦并听说了大灾变的谣言后，就动身前往低语森林。她在和牧师们讨论过后，应该要赶快回来。我们很担心她至今尚未归来的原因。\n 至于你所关心的晋升问题，只有考瑞拥有足够的资格测试你的能力和技巧。如果你找到了她，你应该要提供她相关的协助，并且告诉我们她的状况和目前的情形。
Cauri returned and informed us of this assistance you provided her.\nShe also notified us of your promotion.\nCongratulations, may your profits bring you much joy!	考瑞回来之后，告诉了我们你对她提供的协助。她也通知了我们你的晋升。恭喜，愿你的收获替你带来欢喜！
This is the home of Cauri Blackthorne, greatest Dark Elf warrior.\nShe is currently out, working for the Merchant Council of Alvar.\nPerhaps you should consult them as to her whereabouts.	这是考瑞·黑王座的家，她是最伟大的黑暗精灵战士。她目前不在这里，正在替阿尔瓦商人公会工作。也许你应该去找他们，问问看她人在哪里。

It was the worst of horrors! I was on guard in the main labyrinth just north of here when the waters came. A great wave as high as my head flooded through the passages. I managed to reach this high chamber. I waited for others, but there were none. I fear that many of the herd are dead or trapped inside the lair.	那实在恐怖极了！我当时正在此地北边的主迷宫中站岗。和我人一样高的巨浪突然之间涌进隧道之中。我勉强冲到这个高地，我花了很多时间等待其它人，但他们一直没有出现。恐怕我的同胞有很多人已经去世，或者是还被困在巢穴中。
Balthazar Lair has many levels. There are other chambers as high as this one., so there may be others who have survived.I have thought long on this. Though many of the tunnels are flooded, it may be possible to drain them using the doors and air vents as valves. In fact, the whole lair could be drained through the lower level escape tunnel. Help my herd! You would have our eternal gratitude.	巴尔萨煞巢穴拥有很多不同的高度，有其它的洞穴和这个洞穴一样高，所以可能有很多其它人都活了下来。\n 有关于这次的事件，我想了很久。虽然有很多隧道都已经淹入水中，但是你还是可以利用门和通气管道当做排水的通道。事实上，整个巢穴中的积水可以通过打开底层的逃生管道而把水排干。救救我的同胞！你将会赢得他们永恒的敬意！
I just feel it. There are still survivors of my herd trapped in the lair. If you can figure out how to drain the water from flooded passages, you may be able to reach them. Help them, I beg of you!	我可以感觉到。巢穴中还有活下来的人。只要你能够搞清楚如何把水排出去，你就可以救出他们！求求你们，救救我的同胞！
Balthazar, the greatest leader in the history of our herd, led our warriors in a mighty battle against the Dark Dwarf vermin that erupt from the earth and spread like a plague. It was not a battle that went well for us. Though Balthazar and some warriors escaped, many were lost along with Balthazar's Axe--his regalia of office, and a powerful artifact as well.	巴尔萨煞是我族中历史上最伟大的领袖，他带领着我们和像是病毒一样从地面钻出，并且四处扩散的黑暗矮人搏斗。这场战斗并不顺利。虽然巴尔萨煞和一些战士逃了出来，但是巴尔萨煞之斧（也是他的地位象征）以及许多战士都一起在这场战争中失踪了。
Only Masul, our herd leader, can deem an individual worthy of the title of Minotaur Lord.\nOne way to prove your worth, would be to recover his father's axe, the Axe of Balthazar from the bowels of the Mines of the Dark Dwarfs.\nSet your feet upon the path of this quest, and great is you future!\nBut we must be sure that the axe you find is indeed the Axe of Balthazar.\nTake the Axe to Dadeross, a Minotaur in the service of the Merchants of Alvar, on the island of Dagger Wound.\nHe will be able to verify the truth of what you find.	只有我们目前的领袖马苏尔可以判断一个人到底值不值得获得牛头领主的称号。其中一个获得他青睐的方法是找到他父亲的斧头，也就是遗落在黑暗矮人矿坑之底的巴尔萨煞之斧。只要你出发去完成这个任务，你的未来就光明可期。但是你必须要确认这的确是巴尔萨煞之斧。把你找到的斧头带去给达德洛斯，也就是阿尔瓦商队中的一名牛头人。他就在匕伤群岛上。他将可以判断你所找到的斧头是否货真价实。

Korbu was the most powerful of the Vampire race.\nOver one hundred years ago, he sought something that our kind cannot accept.\nAbsolution.\nSomehow, Korbu began to experience guilt over the need to feed.\nOr perhaps over the act of feeding itself.\nHe could no longer bear the thought of killing in order to survive.\nMany of our elders argued with him.\nHe decided to find a place where he could ponder his existence.\nThus the Crypt of Korbu was constructed with slave labor in the Ravage Roaming region.\n Korbu has not been heard from since he left.	可布是吸血鬼一族中最厉害的人物。在一百年前，他出发去寻找我族所不能接受的一样东西——“宽恕”。不知道怎么搞的，可布开始体验到吸食人血所带来的罪恶感。或许他是对吸血这个动作感到罪恶感。他没有办法再靠着杀害其它人而生存下去。我族中有许多人和他起了争执。他决定要找个地方仔细思索自己存在的意义。因此，他利用残破镇附近的奴隶建造了可布墓窖。从那之后，我们就再也没有听过任何可布的消息了。

Korbu left us after making the "Arrangement" with the necromancers here in Shadowspire.\nWe aid them in any research they are working on, and assist with the defense of their guild.\nIn return we are allowed to feed on the local population without hindrance.	在他和死灵法师达成把我们安置在阴影塔附近的协议之后，可布就离开了。我们协助死灵法师进行任何需要的研究，并且协助他们防卫整个公会。这让我们可以毫不受阻碍地猎食此地的生物。

Bring him to the temple, recover the brazier and return it to me. You want me in your alliance? Well, do this for me, and I will be in your debt.If you have any questions about the Nightshade Brazier ask Thant. It was he who created the thing.	只要我们能够把龙葵灯炉夺回来，阴影塔的防卫就不会再有任何的问题了。我们可以把这场战争带到太阳神庙的大门前。我们将可以将他们通通消灭！\n 由于他们知道这样东西的重要性，牧师们将这样东西放到他们神庙的最深处。戴森·李蓝认为他可以进入这个内殿。你只需要把他带到神庙去，找回那个炉子，带回那个炉子给我。你想要我加入你们的联盟吗？你只要能够替我完成这个任务，我就欠你一次。\n 如果你们对于这个龙葵灯炉还有任何的问题，请去问谭特。是他创造了这个宝物。
I will not join you until the security of the guild is secure. Bring me the Nightshade Brazier from the Temple of the Sun in Murmurwoods. We will talk again of alliance after that is done.	在我们公会的安全获得保障之前，我不会加入你的联盟。请你将太阳神庙中的龙葵灯炉从低语森林带过来给我。在那之后，我们再来讨论有关联盟的这件事情。
Early on our conflict, the Sun Temple attempted to infiltrate our ranks with a spy. Such foolishness--thinking they could so easily deceive a host of dark magicians!It was child's play to unmask their agent--one "Dyson Leland." Since then we have turned him against his former masters, or so it would seem. Frankly, I don't entirely trust in his loyalty. Still, he has proven useful. As long as he does, we will continue to let him live.	在我们的冲突刚开始的时候，太阳神庙派了一名信徒来渗透我们的阵营。真是太愚笨了，他们竟然认为自己可以欺骗一群黑魔法师！\n 要揭穿这个戴森·李蓝的身份实在太简单了。从那之后，我们就让他成了出卖自己同伴的人。至少表面上是这样。说实话，我并不相信这个家伙的忠诚。不过，他依旧有他的用处。至少在这点还成立的时候，我们会让他继续活下去。
How can anyone's quest for power bring him to participate in the horrors of the Dark Path? And yet, here is a whole house of them! Oh, they will call themselves "seekers of knowledge," but these magicians of death are abomination! Loathsome corruption incarnate!Only through my own fear can the foul Necromancers' Guild call itself "my master."	怎么可能有任何人愿意为了力量而忍受黑暗之路的恐怖？但是，这里竟然有这么样的一群人！喔，他们竟然还胆敢叫自己“知识的追求者”，这些家伙根本是种低贱的传染病！寄生虫！\n 只因为我对于自己的恐惧，这个该死的死灵法师公会才能够认为自己是我的主人。
When I was young, all I wanted to do was to join the Order of Light--to be a selfless servant of the good. As soon as I was of age, I entered the Temple of the Sun with a heart filled with the fire of purity.What a fool I was. If there is goodness in the world, I have not seen it. There is certainly none in the halls of the Sun Temple! Hiding behind the righteousness of sanctity, Order members perform the worst crimes of greed and self-indulgence. If it were not so horrid, it would pass for something like irony.	当我还年轻的时候，我满脑子想的都是如何加入光之教派的事情。我想要成为善良之道无私、奉献的仆人。当我到了适当的年纪之后，我的心中充满了洁净的火焰，义无反顾地踏进了太阳神庙。\n 我真是蠢到家了。如果世界上还有任何的善良和正义，我恐怕早就错失了他们。太阳神庙的圣殿中根本没有所谓的善良！这些躲在善良大旗底下的走狗，以善良之名干尽一切肮脏的罪恶、自私、独断。如果不是因为他们的行为太过肮脏，我可能还会觉得这样的状况非常讽刺。
A few months ago, I was called to a private audience with the Sun Temple head priest, Oskar Tyre. He sent me out on a mission of infiltration against the Necromancers' Guild. "Become as they are," he said. "Gain their trust. While you bide your time waiting to deliver a killing blow, feed us their secrets."A too simple plan from too simple a leader! Oh, I did as he said, bathing myself in evil so I might seem evil. But it was as if the guild could see into my heart. I was unmasked as a spy. Under threat of death, I now follow their orders.	几个月之前，太阳神庙的大主教——奥斯卡·提尔秘密召见我。他派我去渗透死灵法师公会。“成为他们的一份子，”他说。“获取他们的信任。当你准备好给予他们致命一击的时候，把秘密告诉我们。”\n 这是一个头脑简单的人想出的简单计划！喔，我的确照着他所说的去作了，尽可能地让自己沐浴在邪恶之中，让自己成为邪恶之人。但是，彷佛公会可以直视我的内心一般，我间谍的身份被揭穿了。他们威胁要杀死我，所以我现在听从他们的命令。
Join with you? An interesting idea. But what can you offer me? After all I have been through I find myself consumed with a thirst for revenge. I would go with you, but am unwilling to spend my efforts lightly. Give me an opportunity to hurt one of my enemies--the cursed Temple of the Sun or the abominable Necromancers' Guild--and I will travel with you gladly.	加入你？这个点子真有趣。但是你能够给我什么呢？在我经历了这一切之后，我心中只剩下了复仇的火焰。我愿意和你一起走，但是我不愿意轻易浪费自己的力气。给我一个机会，让我可以打击我的敌人，不管是该死的太阳神庙或是邪恶的死灵法师公会都好，我就愿意和你一起走！
Now that the Nightshade Brazier covers Shadowspire in its fortifying darkness. Thant goes to lead his minions.	现在，龙葵灯炉已经回来了，阴影塔已经开始在黑暗中统筹军队。谭特得要带领他的部队准备出发了。
Certainly the elemental unrest concerns us greatly. Why one need travel only a short distance north from our temple to see a great maelstrom of unnatural duration and fury. But sadly we have other matters pressing.The sick horror that is the Necromancers' Guild must be blighted from this land! We wage holy war on the evil in Shadowspire. Our entire spirit and power goes into the campaign we wage against Sandro and his minions. Until that is done we cannot entertain participation in your alliance.	元素的骚动的确让我们非常关切。为什么呢？你只要从我们的神庙往北走一小段距离，你就可以看到一个强烈到让人吃惊的漩涡。真可惜，我们目前手上有很紧迫的事情必须处理。\n 死灵法师公会这群该死的寄生虫必须被清除！我们已经对阴影塔地区的邪恶展开了一场圣战。我们的全心全灵都投入了和山德罗以及他手下的战斗中。在这工作完成之前，我们无法加入你的联盟。
Excellent work! With the Skeleton Transformer destroyed, I am more than confident that the light of righteousness will cleanse Shadowspire of evil. The Necromancers' Guild is as good as no more.As I agreed, you may now consider the Temple of the Sun an ally against the elemental cataclysm. I myself will sit on your alliance council in Ravenshore. I will make my departure arrangements with all due haste.	干得好！在摧毁了骷髅转换器之后，我相信我们更有把握可以在光明的照耀之下摧毁阴影塔地区的邪恶。死灵法师公会已经如风中残烛了。\n 如同我之前同意的，太阳神庙现在已经加入对抗元素灾变的阵营。我自己将会前往渡鸦海滨加入议会。我会尽快安排好一切，赶快赶到渡鸦海滨去。
You come here again with talk of alliance? I have told you what you must do. Go to the Necromancers' Guild in Shadowspire, find Dyson Leland and help him to destroy their Skeleton Transformer!	你来这边再度讨论有关联盟的问题？我已经告诉你该怎么做了。赶快去阴影塔地区寻找戴森·李蓝，协助他摧毁那个骷髅转换器！快点！
Then again, we could spare some resources for your alliance if the war would turn in our favor. Perhaps you could be our agent of fortune?Inside the Necromancers' Guild is a device known as the "Skeleton Transformer." It converts living creatures into the skeletons which the dark mages use for the bulk of their reinforcements. If it were destroyed, we would quickly have the upper hand.We have an agent, Dyson Leland, placed in their guild. Find him and help him to wreck their skeleton making device. Do this and I will consider your request more favorably.	不过，如果我们占了上风，我们应该可以派出一些人力和物力协助你们。也许你愿意助我们一臂之力？\n 在死灵法师公会内部，有个被称为“骷髅转换器”的装置。它可以将活生生的生物转换成骷髅，变成黑暗法师们的士兵。如果我们可以摧毁这个装置，我们就可以很快地在这场战争中占上风。\n 我们在公会中安排了一个卧底间谍戴森·李蓝。去找到他，并且协助他摧毁这个制造骷髅的机器。你只要完成这个任务，我就会更认真地考虑你的提议。
Do you take the prophecy so lightly that you shirk the role it has cast for you? These are dire times calling for dire actions from all. Go forth across the land and forge the unity represented by the alliance. Go, unless you favor the oblivion that threatens us!	难道因为你太过轻视预言，导致你忽视了自己在预言中的地位？时机紧迫，我们也必须采用非常手段。赶快统合所有的势力，让他们团结成强大的联盟。快去，除非你对眼前的毁灭视而不见！
Using Arion Hunter's boats we have managed to gather some information. I sent one to Ravage Roaming and another to the Dagger Wound Islands. It is reported that strange portal gateways have appeared in both regions. The gateway in the islands stands on the slopes of the large volcano that rose from the sea southeast of the main island. The Ravage Roaming portal is in the middle of the large lake. It is said that the water which formed the lake flooded out of the gateway.	你可以利用艾瑞安·韩特的船只来收集更多的情报。我派了一艘船去残破镇，另外一艘船去匕伤群岛。根据报告，这两个地区都出现了神秘的传送门。岛上的传送门就出现在主岛东南方的火山山坡上。残破镇的传送门就在一个大湖的中央。据说构成那座湖的水是从传送门之中源源流出的。
Within hours of our first meeting, a most fortuitous development occurred. We were contacted by a great sage, Xanthor, of the Ironfist courts of Enroth!Using his great magic, he projected his image into this very chamber. He and Roland and Catherine Ironfist were at sea off our shores when the cataclysm occurred. They survived the resulting storms and now would like to land in Ravenshore to aid the alliance. Unfortunately, they are blocked by the Regnan pirate fleet. We must help them to land. Xanthor claims much knowledge of what has happened here. We need his council!	就在我们第一次聚会之后几个小时，有件最幸运的事情发生了。恩洛斯的艾恩法斯特王朝中的伟大智者萨索尔和我们联络了！\n 他利用他强大的法力将自己的影像投射在这个房间中。他和罗兰德以及凯瑟琳·艾恩法斯特在大灾变发生的时候，他们正好在我们国度的外海。他们勉强躲过了接下来的大暴风，现在愿意前来渡鸦海滨协助联盟。但很不幸地，他们被雷格那海盗的舰队阻挡在外海上。我们必须要协助他们登陆。萨索尔声称他对于在此地发生的事情十分了解。我们需要他的指引！
Your good work sinking the Regnan fleet has already had the desired results. Just yesterday morning, Catherine and Roland Ironfist arrived along with their sage, Xanthor. With them in the alliance, we are made stronger--immeasurably so.I will admit that the time of this crisis has contained some of my darkest moments. Thanks to your efforts I believe that the worst of this is over. I have hopes that we will indeed survive this.	你弄沉雷格那舰队的完美表现已经获得了相当不错的回报。昨天早上，凯瑟琳和罗兰德·艾恩法斯特和他们的智者萨索尔一起抵达了此地。在我们拥有了他们的协助之后，我们的力量可说是大幅地增加。\n 我必须承认，这次的危机中包含了我自己遭遇到的最黑暗的时刻。多谢你的努力，我相信最糟糕的状况已经过去了。我现在认为我们应该可以成功地逃脱末日的威胁。
You must sink that Regnan fleet! It is imperative that the Ironfists be allowed to land safely. Their sage Xanthor has knowledge of the crisis that we cannot do without.	你一定要弄沉那些雷格那的舰队！艾恩法斯特家族一定要成功进入我们的港口！他们的智者萨索尔对于我们所面对的危机拥有我们所需要的知识！
Our sources believe the main Regnan fleet is in a hidden port somewhere on Regna Island. If you could sink the fleet in dock you could greatly cripple their ability to patrol the seas off our shore. Unfortunately, we don't have a fleet of our own strong enough to make a landing on Regna.Brekish Onefang has gotten message to us that the Regnans have built an outpost on an atoll off the main Dagger Wound Island. He believes this outpost is resupplied by mysterious means. His scouts never see ships land there, but they are always well stocked. Perhaps you should go there and solve the mystery. Perhaps the answer will convey you to Regna?Regardless of the means, the Regnan fleet must be sunk if the Ironfists are to land in Ravenshore.	我们的情报来源相信雷格那海军的舰队就躲在雷格那岛上的某个港口中。如果你可以将港口中的所有船只都弄沉，你将可以大幅破坏他们巡逻邻近海域的能力。很不幸地，我们自己没有足够强大的舰队可以强行登陆雷格那。\n 布拉克许·单牙已经送了一个消息给我们，通知我们雷格那人已经在匕伤群岛的主岛上兴建了一个前哨站。他的哨兵们从来没有看见过任何的船只停靠该处，但是他们的补给却总是很充足。也许你们应该去那边好好调查，解开谜团。也许解答会让你找到前往雷格那的路？\n 不管如何，雷格那的舰队都必须受到重创，艾恩法斯特王朝的船只才能够在渡鸦海滨停靠。
For all their claims of empire and their airs of civilization, the Regnans are the worst scum of Jadame. Why I cannot find myself the least bit surprised that they would take advantage of the crisis to expand their pirating operations. What concerns me most is their new outpost on Dagger Wound. I am sure they mean to use it as a base of attack on the continent. Why, it even puts them in striking distance of Ravenshore!	雷格那人虽然老是夸耀自己的帝国和文明，但他们只不过是贾丹姆大陆上最大群的强盗集团。他们利用这次危机来扩张自己的海盗帝国，我一点也不赶到惊讶。让我最担心的是他们在匕伤群岛上的前哨站。我认为他们一定会利用该处当做攻击主大陆的基地。哼，那里甚至让他们可以直接攻击渡鸦海滨！
I have only met with him briefly, but I am convinced that Xanthor is remarkably knowledgeable about the workings of the elemental planes. Apparently he learned much from the Ironfists' elemental allies during their recent campaigns against the devils of Erathia.Even now he studies the strange crystal in the town square. You may find him there, or perhaps in the nearby residence we have provided him.	我只有和他见过一次面，但是我相信萨索尔对于元素界的一切状况有相当的认知。很明显地他在最近和艾恩法斯特王朝的敌人于埃拉西亚作战的时候，从元素界学到了很多事情。\n 现在他正在研究城镇广场上的那个奇异水晶。你大概可以在那里找到他，他也可能会暂时居住在附近的房屋中。
We of the council have great faith in this Xanthor. He has told me that he is formulating a plan for dealing with the elemental crisis. If anyone can find a solution to our problems, it is he.If you would help us further, be of service to this great sage. Consult with him about the crystal and see what you might do for him.	我们这些议会的成员对于萨索尔拥有相当大的信心。他告诉我们他有个计划，要处理这一切的危机。如果说有任何人可以找到我们所面对问题的解答，那就一定是他了。\n 如果你愿意进一步协助我们，请去帮助那名伟大的智者。询问他有关水晶的问题，看看你能够替他做些什么。
We look to Xanthor to formulate our next actions. Go to him and see how you can help.	我们希望萨索尔能够指引我们下一步该做什么。去找他，看看你能够帮上什么忙。
That we have been joined by the royal court of Enroth has been nothing but good. I was worried they would be afflicted with the need for pomp which infects many other monarchs. I am gladdened to report that this is not the case with this king and queen. They disembarked and set directly to work on our problem. As key members of our alliance, you will find access to them easy. We have put them up in houses here in Ravenshore.	我们获得了恩洛斯皇室的协助带来了非常正面的发展。我本来很担心他们会像一般的皇室一样地骄傲自大，但是他们这对佳偶并不是这样的。他们一来就开始直接协助我们解决问题。由于他们是这次联盟的主要力量之一，你可以轻易地找到他们。我们已经让他们暂时居住在渡鸦海滨的房子中。
From what Xanthor has told us, I'm sure all of the elemental planes are dangerous. Still, I would advise you to take particular caution when exploring the Plane of Fire. Its denizens, according to reports from the Ironsand Desert, are being particularly belligerent.	从萨索尔所告诉我们的事情看来，所有的元素界都已经陷入了危机中。但是，我依旧建议你在探索火元素界的时候要特别小心。根据铁沙漠所送来的报告，火元素界的居民特别地狂野和危险。
All I know of the Plane Between Planes is what I learned from the Alvarian loremasters. Still, if the Destroyer's base is there, proceed with the greatest caution! It is said the plane is made of the stuff of chaos from which all the other planes are formed as if from clay. While chaos has a form, it is fluid. Whatever you see there will not be as it seems. And what is there may be subject to continuous change.	我对界间之地的所知都是从阿尔瓦商人的历史学者口中所得知的。如果毁灭者的基地在那边，你最好小心一点！据说该空间中充满了其它次元创造时所拥有的混沌力量。混沌的外形并不固定。不管你在那边看到什么东西，都不见得会是他真正的形状。该地会不断持续的变化。
What the Alvar loremasters told me of the Plane Between Planes applies here. While I am sure the prisons of the elemental lords are prisons in every sense of the word, I am equally sure that they will not appear as one would expect.	阿尔瓦商人的历史学者所告诉我有关界间之地的事情的确很符合该地的描述。虽然我很确定元素之王的监狱是用来监禁他们的地方，但是我完全不确定他们的外形到底是什么样子。
The Merchant Guild of Alvar owes you a great debt. As a small measure of repayment, we have decided to make this merchanthouse permanently available to you. Please take this gift from us as a token of our profound gratitude for your valorous deeds.	阿尔瓦的商人公会欠你很大的人情。虽然这只是一点小意思，但是我们想要让你可以随时使用这个商人公会之家。请把这个小礼物当做我们对你英勇行为的一点回报。
I know being part of your alliance is important work. Still, I do miss the hunt! I keep imagining all the good fun my men are having while I'm here pushing around papers.Hurry up with this alliance business! I want to get this mess taken care of with dispatch! Without the thrill of the hunt, my life is missing its usual spark.	我知道加入你的联盟是很重要的一件事情。但是我依旧怀念四处狩猎的日子！当我在这边处理许多许多的文档的时候，我不禁会想起同伴们目前在享受的乐趣。\n 快点推动联盟的事物！我想要赶快脱离这一切！没有了狩猎的趣味，我的生命就变得非常无趣。
Judging from the tactics employed by the Regnan pirates, I suspect that Blackwell Cooper is involved. Cooper is an old…shall we say, "associate," of mine. Why, if I were a gambling man, I'd bet gold that he is leading the Regnan expeditionary force on Dagger Wound.	从雷格那海盗所运用的战术看起来，我想黑井·古伯可能有牵涉在里面。古伯是……我应该这么说吧，他是我的一个“朋友”。如果我有赌钱的习惯，我会压注在他率领匕伤群岛上的雷格那部队这件事情上。
I know I hold the council's minority opinion on the subject, but really, I don't see why we have to involve some charlatan magician in this matter! Surely this crisis is solvable, like most things, with judicious application of a length of well-forged steel.	我知道我的意见在议会中算是少数，但是说实话，我实在看不出来干嘛要找个江湖卖艺人来教导我们拯救世界？！危机的确是可以解决的，一切都有解决之道，只要你手中拥有够强的武器，没有不能够解决的问题。
Fah! If you ask me, this idea of Xanthor's is foolhardy! I say if we're facing attack by elemental armies, we should be raising an army to meet them. Frankly, all this talk of prophecy leaves me uneasy. This council is too much under the sway of the Ironfist's pet wizard. I say we take the fight to the elemental planes and introduce the invaders to the sharp edges of our blades!	啐！如果你问我，这个萨索尔所说的话根本是放屁！我认为我们应该直接面对元素界的攻击，我们应该筹组一个军队去打击他们。说实话，这些什么预言的鬼东西让我感到浑身不自在。这个议会根本受到艾恩法斯特王朝的巫师所操纵。我认为我们应该直捣元素界，让那些该死的入侵者尝尝我们的刀锋！
I tell you, this Xanthor is sending you on a wild goose chase. "Plane Between Planes," indeed! What hogwash! The worst of it is that we make no preparations for war in the event the crazy scheme fails, which I expect it will. This mad following of prophecy flies in the face of good military sense!	我告诉你，萨索尔只是派你出去抓瞎而已！“界间之地”，真是无稽之谈！最要命的是，如果这些疯狂的计划失败了，我们根本没有做好作战的准备。而且我认为这些计划注定会失败。照着预言办事，在军事战术上来说简直是胡扯蛋！
I have had more of my share of doubts about this Xanthor, but it is the mark of a man how readily he admits his errors. It is clear to me now that he has given nothing but good and effective advise. If he tells you to rescue the elemental lords, then that is what you must do. Go! And good luck!	我对于这个叫做萨索尔的家伙的确有一些怀疑，但是他愿意承认自己错误的态度让我比较放心。对我来说，他目前所给的建议都是有效的建议。如果他告诉你去拯救元素之王，那么你就应该这样做。快走吧！祝你好运！
Good to see you again, Heroes of Jadame. A good job there, saving the world, and all. You certainly have my thanks, and I'm sure, the thanks of many.I'm sure you've got plenty of other opportunities at this point, but let me know if you want a job hunting Dragons. Probably not much of a challenge for the likes of you, but the offer stands!	贾丹姆大陆的英雄，真高兴再度见到你们。你们拯救全世界的表现实在太好了。我非常感激你们，我相信其它的人也是一样。\n 我很确定你现在应该拥有很多的工作机会。不过，如果你对猎龙有兴趣，请记得跟我说一声。也许这对你这种人来说不是什么大挑战，但是请记得有我的这个承诺！
Small ones, hurry with your alliance preparations! My patience with this land lacking in caves and prey grows dangerously thin. Not only must I sleep in a barn, these people do not even raise cattle. I must live off of fish! Fah!If you value my good temper, be quick!	小家伙，快点去料理你口中联盟的事情！我对于这块土地的关切由于没有洞穴和猎物，已经开始渐渐消退了。我现在不只必须睡在谷仓里面，这些家伙甚至连牛也不养。我还必须靠着吃鱼过活！啐！\n 如果你知道最好不要惹我生气，那动作就给我快一点！
Reaching Regna Island is no small challenge. One of my scouts has reported to me that Regnan boats lie thick on the sea. He also said he saw that the Regnans have a craft that can travel beneath the water. It is said they supply their Dagger Wound outpost with the craft. Perhaps you can use it to reach Regna. The pirates will either miss your passage or take you for one of their own.	要前往雷格那岛并不简单。我的一名斥候回报，海上雷格那人的活动相当频繁。但是他也回报了目击雷格那人有可以在水面下潜航的船只。据说他们利用这艘船只补给匕伤群岛上的前哨站。也许你可以利用这艘船来抵达雷格那。这些海盗可能会因此无法发现你的行踪，或者是把你当做他们的一份子。
I know this is not the time to think of revenge, but I would pass onto you something I learned about my enemy, Charles Quixote. The Minotaur king, Masul, mentioned that Quixote keeps a sword of great power with him in his keep. It is called, "Snake." Perhaps your acquiring of it would help the alliance cause?	我知道这不是想要复仇的时候，但我愿意把我对敌人的了解跟你分享。牛头人的国王马苏尔提到，据说吉柯德在要塞里面放了一把被称为“蛇”的神剑。也许这柄剑可以协助联盟的工作？
My Dragon patrols have located two of the elemental gateways. One lies on the side of a large volcano southeast of the largest Dagger Wound Islands. The other is located in the center of the large lake in Ravage Roaming. I know not where they lead, but you will have to cross a lot of water to reach either.	我的飞龙巡逻队们已经找到了两个元素界的传送门。岛上的传送门就出现在主岛东南方的火山山坡上。残破镇的传送门就在一个大湖的中央。我不知道他们的确实位置，但是你都必须要跨越很多的水才能够抵达这两座传送门。
An Erathian Dragon visited my cave shortly before you arrived there. He reported to me that a new type of Dragon, one seemingly made of crystalline rock, has been seen in Erathia. I'm not sure why I tell you this. Perhaps the giant crystal in the town square reminds me of the story.	在你们抵达这里不久之前，一名埃拉西亚的飞龙来我的洞穴里面拜访我。他告诉我在埃拉西亚上出现了一种新的龙类，一种似乎是由水晶所构成的飞龙。我不知道为什么我要告诉你这件事情。但也许城镇广场上的那座巨大的水晶让我想起这件事情。
Interesting that the Destroyer himself sends you to undo his works. I guess I am not surprised. The workings of the greater Powers are mysterious at best.	真有趣，毁灭者竟然会派你出去解决他们造成的危机。我想我并不会觉得惊讶。超凡力量的行为模式总是让人难以理解。
You have done this land and Dragonkind a great service. For this, we owe you. Know that we will tolerate your presence in our caves, further, we will not hunt you as prey.	你替这块大地和我们龙类都作了非常好的服务。我们非常感激你，为了这样，我们容许你进入我们的洞穴中，不会将你当做猎物看待。
Did you know that our temple in Murmurwoods is the first in this land of Jadame? It is our mission to bring the Light to all corners of the world--even here. We were surprised to see that our enemies, the Necromancers' Guild had preceded our arrival with their own. But no matter, they will soon be dealt with.Say, don't you think this merchant house would make a fine Sun Temple? I will bring it up with Fellmoon after the crisis is over.	你知道我们在低语森林的神庙是贾丹姆大陆上的第一座吗？我们的任务是将光明带到世界上的每个角落，即使在这里也不例外。我们很惊讶看见死灵法师公会竟然比我们先到了。不过没关系，我们会料理他们的。\n 你觉不觉得这座屋子将来可以作为太阳神庙？当这次危机结束之后，我会跟费尔蒙提这件事情的。
If you're off to Regna, you might want to look for the Mace of the Sun. When my brothers and I made the crossing from Erathia, we were attacked by the Regnan fleet. All but one of our ships escaped. Sadly, the lost ship was the one carrying the mission leader, Brother Howe. He had the weapon in his possession. I saw Brother Howe slain by the pirates, but perhaps they still have the mace.	如果你准备去雷格那，你可以会想要先找到烈阳钉锤。当我和弟兄们从埃拉西亚渡海过来的时候，我们受到了雷格那舰队的攻击。我们只有一艘船没有逃出来。很遗憾地，那艘船就是该次任务的领导者豪威弟兄的旗舰。他带着我之前所提到的武器。我目击了豪威弟兄被海盗杀死，但也许他们还保留着那柄武器。
I am pleased with these Ironfists and their sage, Xanthor. Though we have never worked together directly, their work in Enroth and Erathia against the Necromancers' Guild and the devils have clearly marked them as servants of The Light. I have full faith in their plans for saving us from the crisis.	我对于艾恩法斯特家族和他们的智者萨索尔加入的这件事情感到很高兴。虽然我从来没有和他们直接合作过，但是他们在恩洛斯和埃拉西亚上打击死灵法师公会和恶魔的成就确实使他们成为了光明使徒的典范。我对他们解决危机的计划充满信心。
The Sun Temple has had much time to study the gateway which appeared in Murmurwoods. I am positive it leads to the Plane of Air. On several occasions my brothers have seen air spirits around the gateway. Some of the braver have peered through it and have reported seeing a whirling realm of clouds.	太阳神庙有很多的时间研究出现在低语森林中的传送门。我很确定它是通往风元素界的通道。我的弟兄们有几次曾经亲眼目击到有风元素从传送门离开。有些更勇敢的人们甚至曾经从传送门往内看过，他们发现了一个里面有云层快速卷动的世界。
As a Priest of the Light, I have my own ideas about the source of the crisis. I am positive that either the Temple of the Moon or the Necromancers' Guild is somehow involved. Great Light! What if they've banded together? What a horrid thought.	身为光明之道的牧师，我对于危机有自己的定义。我很确定月亮神庙或是死灵法师公会有牵扯进去。天哪！万一他们联合起来怎么办，这真是太可怕了！
I'm not sure you can trust the words of this so called, "Destroyer." Certainly he is not the Destroyer of scripture. And all this talk of Kreegans and men living on many worlds. It flies in the face of the Truth my temple has shepherded from the beginning of time!	我不太确定我们是否可以相信这个所谓毁灭者所说的话。他一定不可能是圣书中所说的毁灭者，而他所谓的恶魔和人们居住在许多世界的说法更是完全与我们这座神庙所坚持的理念相违背！
It is good to have you with us, Heroes of Jadame. Know that the Temple of the Sun also considers you servants of the Light. You are always welcome among us.	贾丹姆大陆的英雄，有你们在这里真好。太阳神庙认为你们也是光明的使徒。我们永远都欢迎你们。
I wish my first visit to Ravenshore was made under better circumstances. This town has a lot to offer and I wish I had time to explore it more. On the bright side, this merchant house has a great library. Have you seen it?	真希望我来拜访渡鸦海滨的第一次旅程能够在比较不一样的状况下。这座城镇有很多有趣的事情，我真希望自己有时间多逛逛。不过，至少这座商人之家拥有非常大的图书馆。你去过那边了吗？
The Regnans have built and outpost on one of the Dagger Wound Islands. We fear they may attack Ravenshore. If this happens, we will evacuate the city. We will return once the garrison has rid Ravenshore of pirates.	雷格那人在匕伤群岛上已经建造了一个前哨站。我们担心他们会从那边攻击渡鸦海滨。如果发生了这种悲剧，我们就必须先疏散渡鸦海滨中的居民才行。在渡鸦海滨的守军赶走了海盗之后，我们会再度回来的。
When I became herd leader, I never expected my reign would be so full of rich experience. Certainly I never thought I'd meet anyone like Queen Catherine! Did you know that until she abdicated her rule in Erathia, she was ruler of both it and Enroth?	当我成为领袖的时候，我并没有意料到我的统治会带来这么多的经验。我根本没想过我会遇到凯瑟琳皇后！你知道吗？在她逊位之前，她本来是埃拉西亚和恩洛斯的统治者！
As you know, the gateway leading to the Plane of Water appeared near Balthazar Lair in Ravage Roaming. It lies in the middle of the lake created by the great flood that poured forth from it.	如同你知道的一样，通往水元素界的传送门出现在残破镇的巴尔萨煞巢穴，也就是在一个大湖正中央。
You may have difficulty finding supplies in the lands beyond the crystal. When I left Balthazar Lair, I brought with me a quantity of the iron rations carried by my armies on campaign. Here, take what I have. They are light and nourishing. I hope you find them useful.	在水晶之后的世界中你可能会很难找到足够的补给。当我离开巴尔萨煞巢穴的时候，我带了一些干粮，这些是我的部队和族人旅行时携带的粮食。它们很轻，营养也很充足。我希望能够帮上你的忙。
I have no more rations to give. If you need more, you'll have to find food elsewhere.	我已经没有干粮可以给你了。如果你需要更多，你得去别的地方找才行。
Why if the imprisonment of the elemental lords is what started this crisis, then surely releasing them is the way to end it! They are beings of almost unimaginable power. Once freed of the Destroyer's control, they would be able to set things right again. This must be what the prophecy speaks of!	如果元素之王的被禁锢导致了这一切的灾变，那么，释放他们当然会结束这一切！他们是拥有强大力量的生物。一旦他们脱离了毁灭者的控制，他们一定可以把事情重新处理好的。这一定就是预言所提到的状况！
It cannot be said enough. My herd as well as all the beings of Jadame are forever in your debt. Thanks to your good work here and in the planes, Balthazar Lair can once again know greatness. You and your comrades are welcome here any time you wish to visit. A blessing on you all!	我们对你的感激真是无比的深重。我的同胞和贾丹姆大陆上所有的生物都是你所拯救的。多谢你在此地和在元素界中的努力，巴尔萨煞巢穴又有了继续发展的机会。你和你的同志们随时都可以来拜访我们，我们永远欢迎你们。
I can't help feeling that I could be doing better work elsewhere. I'm sure Thant can handle operation of the guild in my absence, but still, sitting here on my hands while we are at war with the Sun Temple tries my patience. You really must be quicker about your duties!	我实在忍不住要想：如果我在其它地方会不会比较有用。我很确定谭特可以替我掌管死灵法师公会，但是，当我们在和太阳神庙作战的时候，我竟然不能够亲自带领军队，实在让我很担心。你的动作最好快一点！
When one thinks of the Regnans, the phrase "masters of magic" doesn't normally come to mind. But I caution you, the Regnan pirates have held their island for many centuries. They have had much time to both acquire and study arcane knowledge. Some powerful magicians live there. If you must face them in combat, be prepared for anything.	当我们想到雷格那人的时候，很少人会认为他们是魔法的大师。但是我必须要提醒你一点，雷格那人已经控制了他们的岛屿许多年，拥有足够的时间和资源去研究魔法方面的知识。有许多厉害的法师居住在该处。如果你必须和他们战斗，请做好准备。
It is not true that we of the Necromancers' Guild do not respect mages of other schools. That they have chosen paths leading to lesser powers is of their own concerns. This Xanthor is formidable for a wizard not of the Dark Path. As much as is possible, I find his advise, passably worthy.	我们死灵法师公会其实是很尊重其它学派法师的。他们自己愿意选择低下的力量作研究是他们自己的意愿。以一个不属于黑暗界的法师来说，萨索尔算是相当厉害的家伙。就我来看，我想他的建议还算是可以接受。
I have learned that the Dark Dwarves of Alvar hold an item I have long coveted, but may be of particular use to you if you when you travel among the elemental planes. The Ring of Planes conveys upon its wearer powerful wards against damaging spells of earth, water, air, and fire.	我听说阿尔瓦的黑暗矮人手中有一个我已经寻求很久的物品，也许可以协助你在元素界之间旅行。次元之戒可以让持有者对于地、水、风、火的攻击拥有相当的抵抗力。
In my studies I have learned something of this Plane Between Planes. It is written that its denizens are not born in the normal fashion. I have heard that creatures can be found there which manifest the energies of the nightmare dreams of humankind. I have little more information, but I imagine such a creature would be both powerful and malevolent.	在我研究的过程中，我对于这个界间之地有了一些认知。居住在其中的生物并非是通过一般的方式诞生的，我听说在那边的生物是通过收集人类恶梦的能源而创造出来的。我没有更进一步的信息，但是，我认为这样的生物应该拥有相当可怕的力量。
A basic fact of magic is that Fire opposes Water, and Air opposes Earth. Within these parings, the one element has weaknesses regarding its opposite element. I expect the Destroyer exploits this principle in the construction of his prisons.	魔法的基本常识是：火和水互克、风和地互克。在这些配对中，每个元素都有一些对应元素可以压制的弱点。我预料毁灭者将会照着这样的相生相克来建造他的监狱。
I know I speak for the guild when I say we owe you a debt of gratitude. I discharge this debt by granting you and your companions safe passage through all lands under our control. No guild member will kidnap you for arcane experimentation, nor will any member consider you feed for his servants, living or otherwise. This guild edict will be in effect for a period of one year from today.And of course, you have my personal, thanks.	当我说我们很感激你的时候，我代表的是全公会。我现在给予你在公会管辖下所有土地自由通行的权力，以便还你这个人情债。没有任何的公会成员会绑架你进行魔法实验，也没有任何公会成员会让你成为他麾下使徒的食物。这个公会的饬令将会有一年的有效期。\n 当然，我本人也对你非常感激。
The master is not here at present. He went to consult with Elgar Fellmoon at the council chamber. Perhaps you will find him there.	大人现在不在，他去议会大厅和艾尔加·费尔蒙讨论事情了。也许你可以在那边找到他。
The crystal placed by the Destroyer in the town square serves two functions. One, if we do nothing, it will destroy the world. Two, it acts as a gateway to another realm of existence--the Plane Between Planes--where the Destroyer most certainly has his base.The crystal calls forth the elemental forces into our world and draws them to it. When these forces meet here at the crystal, the world will be destroyed in cataclysm. If we are to prevent this someone will have to use the crystal as a gateway to reach the Destroyer and either convince him to end his assault, or eliminate him.	毁灭者在城镇广场上放置的水晶有两个主要用途。第一，如果我们袖手旁观，它将可以摧毁整个世界。第二，这是一个通往另一个次元——界间之地——的传送门，很明显，界间之地就是毁灭者的基地。\n 这个水晶将元素界的力量召唤进我们世界中，并且吸引他们往这个水晶的方向集中。当这些力量在水晶中会合的时候，整个世界将会被大灾变给摧毁。如果我们想要阻止这一切，一定得有人通过这个传送门去寻找毁灭者，说服他终止这样的行为，或是消灭他。
Since the crystal is attuned to the elemental forces, it will only pass through those who are themselves so attuned. I believe it is possible to create a key that will simulate attunement for a small group such as yourselves. I can make the key, but require components of pure elemental forces to do so.Such components exist in only one form that I know of. On each of the four elemental planes, you will find gemstones called the "elemental hearts." Bring these to me and I will build the key to open the crystal gateway.	由于这个水晶会和元素之力共振，所以他只容许拥有元素之力的人通过。我认为可以制造一个模拟元素共振的钥匙，让像你们这样的一小群人通过。我可以制造这把钥匙，但是我需要拥有纯粹元素之力的物品来制造它。\n 这种物品我只知道以一种方式存在于这个世界。在每个元素界中，你们将可以找到一种被称为元素之心的宝石。将这些东西带给我，我将可以制造打开水晶传送门的钥匙。
I am glad that you have had some measure of success in retrieving the elemental hearts, but what you have is not enough. I need one from each of the four elemental planes before I can build the key to the crystal gateway. Bring me the rest of the hearts so we can proceed.	我很高兴你找回了部分的元素之心，但是，要成功的打造钥匙，我必须要有全部的四枚元素之心才行。把其它的元素之心带给我，这样我才可以继续我的工作。
To go through the crystal gateway and confront the Destroyer, you need the key I must build! To build this key, I need the elemental heart gemstones from the four elemental planes! Go and get these for me!	要穿过水晶传送门，面对毁灭者，你将会需要我的钥匙。而我需要四个元素界中的元素之心才能打造这个物品！快点去把它们找来给我！
Something about this crystal seems to put a compulsion on the denizens of the elemental planes. Why else would they make war on our plane? I have heard yours and other first hand reports. It seems almost as if these elementals are driven by a madness.And madness it is! For destroying Enroth would create a mighty planar unbalance which would certainly victimize their own planes. They might even conceivably be themselves destroyed in the cataclysm. At the very least, they risk changing the nature of their own planes. I cannot see how such change could possibly be for the better!	这个水晶似乎让元素界的生物感应到了一种奇怪的力量。否则他们干嘛在我们的世界上掀起战火？我已经看过你们的第一手报告，看起来似乎这些元素都被一种疯狂的力量所影响。\n 这的确是非常的疯狂！因为摧毁恩洛斯将有可能在元素界之间造成不平衡的状态，危及他们自己的空间，他们甚至可能在这场大灾变中把自己也给卷入。即使在最乐观的状况下，他们所居住空间的也会受到影响。我根本不明白，这样的改变怎么可能带来任何的好处？
That the elemental lords are imprisoned on the Plane Between Planes explains much. I had wondered why the denizens of the Earth, Air, Water, and Fire Planes have become so hostile. Usually, they are content to keep to their own place in the order. I suppose it was necessary for the Destroyer to take the lords from their homelands. Without the lords calming presence, it would be much easier for him to compel the elementals as he has.It is important for you to free the elemental lords. They can restore order to their planes and prevent the cataclysm!	元素之王被囚禁在界间之地元的事实证明了一切。我一直怀疑为什么地水风火元素界的生物会变得这么狂暴。因为在一般的状况下，他们通常只喜欢居住在自己的空间中。我认为毁灭者必须要将这些元素之王绑架后，才能让他更为轻易地操纵这些元素生物。\n 你一定要救出这些元素之王才行。只有他们能够逆转大灾变，阻止末日的发生！
Yes, I have heard the name, "Escaton." He is variously called in the legends of many cultures. I know not his nature, but it is his purpose to destroy worlds.Yes, "worlds," I say. More and more evidence points to the conclusion that Enroth is not the only place in this universe where there is life. Tales from before the Silence speak of many living among the stars. Yes, it sounds impossible to think that one could live on a light in the sky, but consider that these stars may be like our sun. If this is so, then why can't these suns shine on other places like Enroth? If this was the case, then there could be a "destroyer of worlds." No! Someone must remain here to guard the lair against the barbarian raiders. Come back after you've helped my herd. I may consider joining you then.	不行！一定有人得留在这里，守卫巢穴不受野蛮人的入侵。在你拯救了我的同胞之后再回来找我，那时我也许可以加入你们。
Yellow Fever is a very crippling disease that pops up every few years.\nUsually we arrange for medicine from the mainland, but now that the bridges are destroyed, no one can make it to the docks!	黄热病是种会让人残障的传染病，每隔几年就会突然冒出来。通常我们会从主大陆那边进口药物，但是在桥梁被摧毁之后，没有人可以过去买药了！
Here, take these scrolls of Cure Disease.\nMaybe we can at least prevent an epidemic!\nThe six huts on the outer islands are infected.\nIf the teleporters between the islands are repaired, you can take these scrolls to the huts.\nUnfortunately you will have to find three more scrolls in your travels.	来，收下这些治愈疾病的卷轴。也许我们至少可以阻止传染病的散布。在外岛的六座小屋都被感染了。如果在各岛屿之间来往的传送门被修复了，你可以把这些卷轴送到那些小屋去。很不幸的，你必须要在旅途上再找到额外三个卷轴才够你使用。

Black Potions are made of a complex blending of many of the three basic alchemical reagents. Red reagents include Widowsweep Berries, Wolf's Eye, and Phials of Gog Blood.\nSome blue reagents are Phoenix Feather, Phima Root, Meteor Fragment and Will O' Wisps Heart.\nAnd some yellow reagents are Datura, Dragon Turtle Fang, Poppy Pods and Thornbark.	黑色药剂是由混合许多基本的药材所获得的。红色的药材包括了柳条浆果、狼之眼、垢鬼血。蓝色的药剂包括了火凤羽、费玛根、陨石片、鬼火心。黄色药材则包括了曼陀罗、龙龟牙、罂粟茎和刺木皮。

For years the Guild of Necromancers has searched for the Bone of Doom.\nThe powerful artifact would be of great use to them if recovered.\nIf you were to find this item and bring it to me, you will be rewarded beyond your wildest dreams.	死灵法师公会已经找了毁灭之骨好一阵子了。这个威力强大的物品将会让他们发展很多新的法术。如果你能够替我找到这样宝物，并且将它带来给我，我将会给予你梦想中最庞大的奖励！
In times almost forgotten a powerful Necromancer named Zacharia almost dominated all of Jadame.\nHis mastery of the dark arts of necromancy and the elemental magics were combined with an unnaturally strong afinity for Dark energy. His legions of undead helped him enslave all peoples, including the Dark Elves of Alvar.\nA plan was made by the Temple of the Sun to defeat Zacharia.\nUtilizing the combined might of all of the Clerics in the Temple they forged the sword named Solkrist.\nThe Patriarch of the Temple led the legions of the Sun against the forces of Zacharia.\nThe battle raged for years after which the forces of the Temple of the Sun found themselves at the base of the dreaded Shadowspire.\nThere the Patriarch battled Zarcharia in single combat. At the end of the battle both the Patriarch and Zacharia lay dead.\nThe Patriarch had cut the left arm from Zacharia's body just as the Necromancer's last spell stopped the Patriarch's heart.\nThe power of the sword Solkrist instantly slew the Necromancer.\nSolkrist disappeared forever in the mayhem after the fight, as did the arm of Zacharia.\nThe bone from this arm was so instilled with such Dark Magic that it was called the Bone of Doom.	在几乎已经被众人所遗忘的过去，有一名强大的死灵法师萨查力亚几乎征服了全贾丹姆大陆。他对于黑暗类魔法和元素系魔法的研究都结合在黑暗力量的统御之下。他的不死军队协助他奴役所有的种族，包括了阿尔瓦的黑暗精灵。太阳神庙设法规划出攻击萨查力亚的战术，他们结合了太阳神庙中所有牧师的力量，创造出了一柄名为太阳圣剑的武器。神庙的大主教率领着太阳军团队抗萨查力亚的军队。这场战争持续了数年之久，最后太阳军团发现自己已经进入了恐怖的阴影塔地区。太阳神庙的主教一对一地单挑萨查力亚。在激战之后，俩人都壮烈战死了。正当萨查力亚的最后一个法术发出，让主教死亡之前，主教正好将萨查力亚的右臂给砍了下来。太阳圣剑的力量当场就杀死了萨查力亚。之后太阳圣剑和萨查力亚的右臂都同样消失在战后的混乱中。这个手臂中的骨头吸收了极为强大的黑暗力量，因此又被称作毁灭之骨。

Tobersk fruit is native only to the Dagger Wound Island region.\nHere the mild winters and humid summers provide the perfect conditions for the Tobersk fruit to achieve the correct levels of sugar and juice.\nThe pulp masters in Ravenshore pay good money for the tobersk fruit.	只有匕伤群岛会出产托伯斯克果。在此地，温和的冬天和潮湿的夏天让托伯斯克果可以成长到拥有足够的甜度和汁液。渡鸦海滨的商人愿意付重金来购买托伯斯克果。\nThe pulp masters in Ravenshore pay good money for the tobersk fruit.

Tobersk fruit is processed in Ravenshore into tobersk pulp.\nIts from this pulp that the master brewers in Alvar distill tobersk brandy.	托伯斯克果是在渡鸦海滨处理成托伯斯克果汁。阿尔瓦的商人会从这些果汁中提炼出酒来。\nIts from this pulp that the master brewers in Alvar distill tobersk brandy.

Ulbrecht Pederton single handedly stopped the Second Pirate War.\nHe charged down the beach to come to Ravenshore's defense as the pirates began to land.\nHe manipulated the sea itself and forced the Regnan fleet back into the ocean.\nUnfortunately this effort cost him the ability to use magic altogether.\nHe still, however, teaches the knowledge of Expert Water Magic.\nHe lives in Ravenshore to this day.	乌尔布拉契·派德东单枪匹马地阻止了第二次的海盗战争。他冲到海边，正好雷格那海盗开始登陆。他呼唤海水的力量，逼迫雷格那舰队退回到海中。很不幸地，这次的牺牲让他失去了使用魔法的能力。不过，他目前还是专家级的水系魔法老师，居住在渡鸦海滨这座城市中。

Tugor Arin, Master of Body Magic, practices his healing skills in the service of the knight Charles Quixote in the Garrote Gorge region.\nHis skills in Body Magic are put to good use healing the unfortunate young knights who are wounded in the crusade against the Dragons of Garrote Gorge.	土加·爱林是肢体魔法的大师级教师，目前居住在绞刑峡谷的猎龙人营地中。在那边，他的肢体医疗魔法对于不幸被飞龙所伤害的年轻骑士来说非常有用。
Grand Master Critias Snowtree, dwells in the peace of the Murmurwoods forest away from the trials and troubles of the world.\nOnly he can instruct you in the ways of Body Magic and raise you to Grand Mastery of this arcane skill.	肢体魔法的宗师克理塔斯·雪树就居住在低语森林中，远离尘世的喧嚣和吵杂。只有他可以教导你如何达到肢体魔法的宗师级地位。
Archibald Dawnsglow, Expert instructor of Light Magic, practices his arts in the city of Ravenshore on the Ifarine Sea.\nHis parents gave his name not knowing that it was the same name as the leader of the Erathian Necromancers' Guild.\nArchibald desires to have his own name made in the Light, not in the darkness of his namesake.	阿基巴德·曙光是光明魔法的专家级教师，目前正在伊法力海岸边的渡鸦海滨传授他的技巧。他的父母给了他这个名字，却不知道这刚好和埃拉西亚的死灵法师公会首领的名字一样。阿基巴德希望能够听到自己的名字在光明的历史上被人所传诵，而不是被黑暗界的怪物们礼拜。
Master Lunius Dawnbringer, dwells among the the Clerics of Murmurwoods.He can promote you to Master of Light Magic, if you can survive the dangers of the forest.	卢理那斯·破晓者是光明魔法的大师，他目前居住在低语森林，靠近太阳神庙的地方。如果你能够穿越危险的森林，他就能够让你成为光明魔法的大师。
Aldrin Cleareye, Grand Master of the Light, makes his home on the island of Regna.\nHe hopes to bring an end to the Pirates evil ways.\nHe can instruct you to Grand Masterey, if you survive the Pirates.	艾德林·明眼是光明魔法的宗师，目前居住在靠近雷格那岛的地方。他希望能够让海盗们不要再继续行邪恶之道。如果你能够躲过海盗的防卫线，你就可以让他教导你宗师等级的技巧。
Patwin Darkenmore, Expert teacher of the ways of Dark Magic, lives among the Dark Elves of Alvar.	帕特温·达肯摩尔是暗黑魔法的专家级教师，他目前居住在阿尔瓦的黑暗精灵城市中。
Dark Magic Master Carla Umberpool followed the travels of Sithicus Shadowrunner, Grand Master of the Dark, and makes her home with the Necromancers of Shadowspire.	暗黑魔法的大师卡拉·安伯普尔居住在阴影塔附近的城市中。她和她的老师一样四处旅行，不过目前暂时定居下来，和死灵法师们切磋技巧。

Kyra Sparkmen, Expert teacher of the Identify Item skill, lives in the city of Alvar.	其拉·史巴克曼可以教导学生专家级的物品鉴定术，他目前居住在阿尔瓦城中。

Klain Scarwing, is second in strength only to Deftclaw Redreaver himself.\nKlain holds the title of Grand Master Dragon and instructs all Dragons in the use of their natural abilities.\nSee him if you wish to attain Grand Master status.\nHe dwells here in the Dragon Caves of Garrote Gorge.	克兰·疤翅居住在绞刑峡谷的龙穴中，他可以教导飞龙宗师级的技能。如果你想要获得宗师级的地位和知识，请去找它。

Greetings Novice Bounty Hunter! The Guild of Bounty Hunters serves the public by paying qualified individuals a fee for eliminating the threat of certain creatures or bandits in the realm of Jadame.\nTo achieve the rank of Journeyman Bounty Hunter you will have to earn a total bounty of 30,000 gold.	赏金猎人公会基于服务大众的精神，他们愿意付给一些符合资格的猎人相当数量的赏金；只要他们能够消灭指定的生物和达到指定的数量。要获得赏金猎人老手的称号，你必须至少赚到三万金币的赏金才行。

You have not gathered sufficient bounties to allow us to promote you!	你还没有获得足够的赏金来让我们晋升你！
You currently hold the rank of Master Bounty Hunter! This is the highest rank of our guild.	你目前是赏金猎人大师，这是我们公会最崇高的称号了！

To be declared Arcomage Champion, you must win a game of Arcomage in every tavern on, in, and under the continent of Jadame.\nThere are 11 such taverns sponsoring Arcomage events.\nWhen you have accomplished this, return to me to claim the prize.	要成为“魔幻牌”的霸主，你必须在贾丹姆大陆地面上、下的所有酒吧里面赢得一次的胜利。有十一个酒吧有举办“魔幻牌”的竞赛。当你完成了这项壮举之后，回来找我领取奖赏。
Through your delivery of the Nightshade Brazier into their hands, the Necromancers' Guild has come out on top in their war with the Temple of the Sun. The artifact's magic creates a permanent shadow of darkness which surrounds the Shadowspire region. Under this cover, vampires defend the land both day and night. This has allowed the guild to field larger forces of skeletons, zombies and other undead against the temple's armies. Each report I receive puts the heart of the battle closer and closer to the Sun Temple's doorstep.	在你把龙葵灯炉送到死灵法师公会手上之后，他们已经在与太阳神庙的战争中占了上风。这个宝物的魔法会创造出永久性的黑暗，环绕着阴影塔地区。在这阴影的屏蔽之下，吸血鬼可以日夜不停地保护这块土地。公会则可以趁机驱使大群的骷髅人、僵尸和其它的不死生物对抗神庙的部队。我所收到的每一份报告都指出这场战争越来越靠近太阳神庙的门槛了。
Because you have destroyed the Necromancers' Guild Skeleton Transformer, the Temple of the Sun has come out on top in their war with the guild. The skeletons created by the device made up the bulk of the guild's reinforcements. Without these, the necromancers' forces crumble before the temple's advance. Each report I receive puts the heart of the battle closer to the Necromancers' Guild doorstep.	因为你已经摧毁了死灵法师公会的骷髅转换器，太阳神庙已经占了战争的上风。这个装置所制造出来的骷髅就无法继续加入战斗。没有了这些力量，死灵法师的部队在牧师的步步进逼下溃不成军。我所收到的每份报告都指出，这场战争越来越接近死灵法师公会的门槛。
Lying as they do on opposite ends of the Light to Dark spectrum, relationships between the Necromancers' Guild and the Temple of the Sun have never been the best, but now they battle openly. Oskar Tyre, head priest of the Temple says a holy vision drove him to declare war on the guild so as to rid Jadame of the taint of darkness.Both groups are relative newcomers to our continent. The guild has set up its foothold in Shadowspire, and the First (and only) Sun Temple of Jadame is in Murmurwoods.	他们就如同光明与黑暗两个光谱上的极端一样，死灵法师公会和太阳神庙之间的关系一直都不好，但现在是他们公开宣战的时段。奥斯卡·提尔宣称他看见了不寻常的启示，督促他将这股污染贾丹姆大陆的力量从世界上彻底消除。\n 两群人都不是在我们的大陆上土生土长的。公会基地在阴影塔区域，第一个，也是唯一的太阳神庙则是在低语森林。
It seems Charles Quixote's Dragon hunting operation in Garrote Gorge has almost completely wiped out Garrote Gorge's Dragon population. I'm sure once their done, those mountains will be safer to travel. Still, I wonder what Jadame will be like without Dragons.	看来查尔斯·吉柯德在绞刑峡谷的狩猎行动几乎已经把飞龙都杀光了。我很确定他们完成任务之后该处会变得比较安全。但是，我也很难想像贾丹姆大陆如果没有了飞龙会是什么样子。
By recovering Deftclaw Redreaver's heir for him, you've allowed him to safely attack Charles Quixote's camp. Dragon hunters operate by isolating members of their prey. They just aren't prepared to face Dragons in number! Needless to say, the camp's production of Dragon goods has dwindled to a trickle. A pity. The guild used to turned a good profit off them.	由于你替达夫克劳·红斧找回了它传承的下一代，你让它可以不受拘束地攻击查尔斯·吉柯德的营地。猎龙人是靠着各个击破的战术来对付他们的敌人。他们根本没办法应付大规模的飞龙攻击！不用说，该处的龙产品数量已经大幅地减少了。真可惜，他们本来都可以靠这项出产赚很多钱的。
The renown Dragon slayer, Charles Quixote of Erathia, has set up a hunting expedition in Garrote Gorge. This is no "safari," in fact, it's the largest operation of this type I've heard of. The hunters have a permanent base from which they sell hides, eggs, baby dragons, and other items. Garrote Gorge has always had a large Dragon population, but I imagine they must be facing extinction--so efficient are Quixote's hunters.	著名的屠龙者，从埃拉西亚来的查尔斯·吉柯德在绞刑峡谷指挥一场规模庞大的狩猎。这可不是什么野猎的旅程。事实上，这是我所听过最大规模的狩猎行动。猎人在峡谷中设立了一个永久性的基地，贩卖龙皮、龙蛋、幼龙和其它的物品。绞刑峡谷自古到今都是大量飞龙聚集的地方。但是吉柯德的猎人效率惊人，我想飞龙们可能已经面对灭绝的命运。
The Minotaurs of Balthazar Lair in Ravage Roaming are a proud culture. Their recent defeat of the Vori Frost Giants has, if anything, made them even more fiercely independent--more sure of their ability to face any task alone. Still, I have told the guild that they would make great allies, both to face the current crisis and later as trade partners.	位于残破镇的巴尔萨煞巢穴的牛头人是个骄傲的文明。他们前一阵子击败沃里的霜雪巨人后变得更加独立。因为他们更加确定自己单独承担任何任务的能力。不过，我已经告诉过公会，他们是非常不错的盟友，不管在面对危机和贸易时都一样。
By rescuing the Balthazar Lair Minotaurs, you have ingratiated them to yourselves and by extension, to our guild. Even through the crisis, trade relations with them have improved immeasurably. Once maters of the cataclysm are resolved, I'm sure we will see a tidy profit--a direct result from your good work.	在你救出了巴尔萨煞巢穴的牛头人之后，你已经让他们对你授与无比的信任。即使在危机结束之后，公会和他们的贸易可能性将会大幅提升。一旦危机解除，我想利润将会大幅提升，这都多亏你的努力。
With the bridges destroyed by the eruption of the mountain of fire, we cannot get to the boat dock!\nWe are cut off from the mainland!	由于我们的桥梁都已经被火山给摧毁了，我们现在没办法回去了！更由于我们没办法抵达港口，因此跟主大陆完全断绝了联络。

Did you know I was their captive for over six years? At first, I thought they were devils from hell, but now I am not so sure. They looked like devils surely, but did not act as demons. No, I think they were but another type of monstrous race, like ogres or goblins. Regardless, devils or not, I am glad the world is rid of them!	你知道我曾经被他们俘虏过六年的时间吗？一开始，我以为他们是地狱来的恶魔，但现在我比较不能确定了。他们看起来的确很像恶魔，但行为却不像。不，我想他们也许只是另外一种怪物种族，就像食人妖或是大耳怪一样。不过，不管他们是不是恶魔，我都很高兴他们已经被杀光了！
Catherine and I have had dealings with these "lords" of the elemental planes. I've never trusted them, and now it seems I am right. They were our recent allies against the Kreegans, but a few months later, we find ourselves at war with them here! In both cases I understand neither their reasons for helping or hindrance. I must say I'm looking forward to returning to my lands of Enroth. It has been so long since I've seen them, or my son, Prince Nicolai. He was just a lad when I saw him last and now he is nearly grown. Sir Humphrey was a good choice for regent, but the land has been too long without its king at the reins of state.	我必须说我很期待回到恩洛斯的家乡。我已经好久没有看过自己的故乡和我的儿子尼可拉王子。当我上次看到他的时候，他只不过是个小孩，但是现在他已经快变成大人了。韩福瑞爵士是个不错的摄政王，但我的故乡已经太久没有国王的管理了。
After we defeated the Kreegans in Erathia, I abdicated my throne into the regenthood of one of my generals, Morgan Kendal. I have left in his capable hands the task of choosing Erathia's new ruler. We were sailing for there when the cataclysm struck, drawing us here.	当我们击败了埃拉西亚的克里根人之后，我将自己的王位让给了我的一位将军，摩根·坎道尔。我让他选择埃拉西亚下一个适任的领袖。我知道这看起来像是很大的牺牲，但我可是恩洛斯的第一个皇后。当大灾变降临的时候，我们正好要回到故乡去，这场天灾地变则是将我们吸引了过来。
The Kreegans? They are no more; I am sure of it. After we put down Lucifer Kreegan, my armies scoured Eofol searching for any signs of their continued existence. We found none. No, the Kreegan infestation of our world is eliminated.	克里根人？我很确定他们已经都被消灭了。在我们杀死了魔王路西法之后，我的部下彻底搜索了他的巢穴，调查任何可能的生命迹象。我们完全没有找到。这个世界上的克里根人已经全都被消灭了。
I fought alongside elementals in our campaigns against the Kreegan. I found them stalwart and loyal. I know Roland disagrees, but I cannot call their hostility here a case of fickleness. No, I think something else drives them to attack us--magic perhaps? You should speak with our sage, Xanthor. He has been studying the Destroyer's crystal. I'm sure he's come up with some theory by now.	我在对抗克里根人的战争中曾经和元素们并肩作战。我认为他们忠诚、服从。我知道罗兰德不同意我的想法，但是我不认为他们的敌意就是多变。不，我认为有其他的因素驱使他们攻击我们，也许是魔法？你应该跟我们的智者萨索尔谈谈。他已经研究毁灭者的水晶一段时间了。我很确定他现在应该已经找到了一些理论依据才对。

You must claim a victory at ALL 11 taverns.\nUntil you do, you cannot be declared Arcomage Champion.	你必须要赢得所有的十一个酒吧中的比赛。在你完成之前，你没有办法成为“魔幻牌”霸主。
Even as a lad I knew I had a special relationship with cheese. Why, my first memory is of nibbling a bit of farmer's cheddar from my mother's hand! From that day I knew it would be my passion to eat cheese, to know all of the endless variety of its tasty wonderment, and to search the world for rare and exotic cheese-stuff. And so have I made my life...and destiny.	从我小的时候，我就知道自己和奶酪有着特殊的关连。我的第一个记忆就是从母亲手上啃食刚从农庄中弄出来的奶酪！从那一天开始我就知道自己对于奶酪会产生强烈的热情，会想要体验一切味觉的极致，踏遍整个世界，只为了寻找各种稀少和珍贵的奶酪。这，就是我的宿命。
With the cheeses you have brought me, I have now eaten all of the world's known cheeses. Now none can say that I, Asael Fromago, am not history's most eminent connoisseur of cheese! None can compare to my glory! Ha-ha-ha…	在吃过你带给我的奶酪之后，我现在已经吃过了所有世界知名的奶酪。现在，除了我之外，没有人可以自称为世界上最伟大的奶酪品尝者了！没有人可以跟我争夺这项荣耀了！哈哈哈……
There are no people more dedicated to cheesecraft than the august cheese masters of Vori. Otherwise known to be rather barbaric, the Vori Frost Giants are true emblems of the potential of civilized development--at least as far as cheese making is concerned. Did you know that they have forty-two different words for "cheese."	这世界上没有比沃里的奶酪大师更投入奶酪制作的人了。虽然沃里的霜雪巨人在一般人的眼光中算是蛮族，但他们有相当的文明发展潜力，至少以他们的奶酪制作来说是这样的。你知道吗？他们拥有四十二种不同口味的奶酪！
I have traveled to these lands to catalog its array of available cheese. My task is nearly complete, but there are yet three cheeses I have yet to sample. These are Frelandeau, Eldenbrie and Dunduck. I would reward highly any who could locate these rare and reputedly tasty culinary gems for me.	我已经走遍了全世界去品尝各式各样的奶酪。我的工作几乎已经完成了，但是我还有三种奶酪没有品尝过。他们是独角兽奶酪、艾顿布理奶酪、登达克奶酪。如果谁能够替我找到这些传说中美味无比的宝物，我一定会给予重酬！
Excellent! Give me the cheeses! I must consume them!Ah….munch munch….frelandeau, exquisite….mmmm, and eldenbrie, wondrously smoky…and hmmm, dunduck…well, that's not very nice, is it?You have made my Jadamean cheese cataloging safari a success! I can die a happy man! You have the humble thanks of a man of cheese. And here is your promised reward!	太好了！把所有的奶酪给我！我一定要吃吃看！\n 啊……嚼嚼……独角兽奶酪，味道太精致了……嗯嗯，艾顿布理，烟熏味相当的不错……登达克……味道就不怎么好了，对吧？\n 你帮我完成了贾丹姆大陆的奶酪品尝之旅！我死也甘愿了！我太感激你了！这是我答应给你的报酬！
Very good, you have found me some of the cheese I seek. But now I have my heart set on a full cheese tasting with all three cheeses eaten at once so I can savor them in comparison. Come back when you have then all. I will not take what you have now. I don't think I could resist sampling what you leave--and then the cheese tasting would be ruined!	很好，你已经找到一部份我要的奶酪了，但是我想要一次品尝所有的奶酪，以便彻底地分辨出他们的味道来。在你找到所有的奶酪之后再来找我。你先暂时把你所找到的奶酪带走，我想我抵抗不了这些奶酪的诱惑，可能会一不小心把他们全都吃光！
You say you will find me the cheeses I desire, but here you are returned, empty-handed! Do not waste my precious time! Bring me cheese--Frelandeau, Eldenbrie and Dunduck! Do not return until you have them!	你说你会找到我要的奶酪，但是你现在竟然空手而回！别浪费我宝贵的时间！把独角兽奶酪、艾顿布理奶酪、登达克奶酪带回来给我！在你找到之前别回来！
The night the stranger came through here was the same night that the crystal erupted in the center of town.\nI only caught a glimpse of the stranger.\nHe didn't appear the potent mage that he was.\nI was not in the town square when he summoned the crystal, but I did see the flash of light that traveled across the sky.\nIt was truly a terrible night.	陌生人出现的那天晚上，也就是这些诡异水晶出现的同一个晚上。我只看见那个陌生人的身影一眼，那看起来似乎没有多大的威胁。当他召唤出水晶的时候，我并不在城镇广场上，所以我并没有看见激射入天空的光芒。这真是恐怖的一夜。
A wanderer came into town.\nSeveral ruffians sought to relieve this traveler of whatever wealth he possibly possessed. The wanderer barely paid any of them much notice.\nWith a small flick of his wrist all fell before him.\nThen he walked to the town square and summoned the giant crystal that you'll find there today.	一个漂泊者走进镇子。有几个流氓想要从他身上抢走一些小东西。但那人根本不理他们。他手腕轻轻一振，所有人就都跌倒在他面前。然后他就走到城镇广场上，召唤来你今天所看到的那个巨大的水晶。\nThen he walked to the town square and summoned the giant crystal that you'll find there today.
There is an ancient saying among the Dark Elf people,\n"a thing lives only as long as the last person who remembers it."\nThe events the night the crystal burst forth into our lives will live for a very long time.\nMany of us saw the events of that night.\nThe mage who summoned forth the crystal said not one word to anyone us.\nWhen he looked upon us, it seemed that all there was in his heart and eyes was pity.	在黑暗精灵中有一句俗语，“必须要有人活着才能够把记忆留存下去。”水晶突然出现在我们镇中央的那个景象将会留存很长的一段时间。我们有很多人都看见了那个景象。召唤来水晶的人并没有跟我们说任何的话。当他看着我们的时候，似乎他的眼中和心中都只有同情和怜悯。
When the mage came into town I didn't think much of it.\nThis is Ravenshore.\nEveryone who travels the roads of Jadame comes through here at some time or another.\nI guess I should have paid more attention to him.\nSeems he caused quite a stir in the town square.	当那个法师来到镇上的时候，我并没有多想。这是渡鸦海滨，任何人在贾丹姆大陆上旅行都几乎会经过的地方。我想我应该更注意他一些的。看起来他似乎在城镇广场上造成了相当的混乱。\nSeems he caused quite a stir in the town square.
The Pirates of Regna own the seas.\nNo one can stand against them!\nSeveral years ago, some fishing boat captains thought that they could sail to Erathia to get help dealing with the pirates.\nWe never saw or heard from them again.	雷格那海盗拥有整个大海。没有人可以对抗他们！几年以前，有一些渔船的船长认为自己可以前往埃拉西亚寻求救兵来对付这些海盗。后来我们却再也没有听过他们的消息。
The Regnans are the worst slime to ever set foot on the shore of Jadame. They keep the peoples of this continent from trading with Erathia and Enroth.\nWhat choice do we have?\nEven the Merchants of Alvar couldn't muster a fleet to deal with the pirates.\nRumor says that there are smugglers around that have found ways around the pirate blockades.	雷格那人是贾丹姆大陆附近海域上最糟糕的流氓。他们让这个大陆上的人们无法和埃拉西亚和恩洛斯的人们贸易。我们有什么选择呢？即使连阿尔瓦的商人公会都无法召集够大的舰队来对付他们。谣传私枭们找到了方法可以躲过这些海盗的阻挡。\nRumor says that there are smugglers around that have found ways around the pirate blockades.
The Regnans call themselves the Empire of the Endless Ocean--trying to make more of themselves than there really is.\nAll they are is bullies and bandits.\nOf course, don't tell them that I said that!	雷格那人称自己属于无尽之海的帝国。这只不过是虚张声势而已。他们只不过是流氓和强盗而已。当然，你千万别跟他们说这句话是我说的！
Over the last few weeks there have been reports of bandits and Ogres robbing travelers on all of the major roads of Jadame. Times just seem to be getting worse.\nBetween the Regnan Pirates and these bandits it seems that no one is safe to travel by any means.	过去几周以来，食人妖和强盗似乎在贾丹姆大陆的各主要干道间四处劫掠。日子真是越来越难过了。海上有雷格那海盗，路上又有这些该死的强盗，我们根本没有任何方法可以旅行嘛！\nBetween the Regnan Pirates and these bandits it seems that no one is safe to travel by any means.
Several of the warriors got together to go take care of the ogres that were camping on the road to Ravenshore.\nThat's when we first had word of the fortress they were constructing to the southwest of here.\nSince the fortress was completed the roads to the south and west have not been safe.	曾经有几名战士成群结队地准备对付那些在渡鸦海滨道路附近兴建要塞的食人妖。这是我们第一次听说他们在西南方建造的要塞。从要塞完成之后，往西和往南的道路就都变得不安全了。\nSince the fortress was completed the roads to the south and west have not been safe.
The Smuggler of Jadame may be a myth, but I think not.\nThe Merchants of Alvar seem to be able to send small amounts of goods away across the sea to Erathia and Enroth.\nYet they own no boats of their own.\n This would seem to support the idea that there really are smugglers.\nNow why they work for the merchants is beyond the likes of me.	贾丹姆大陆的走私者也许被人视作神秘的传言，但我可不这么认为。阿尔瓦商人似乎能够将少量的货物运送到埃拉西亚和恩洛斯去。但是他们没有自己的船只。这看起来似乎证明了走私者的存在。但是，他们为什么要替这些商人工作就让我不能理解了。
Wererats!\nThey are the worst of scum!\nThe pick through our trash at night!\nAlways scurrying around in the dark!\nNever seen when the sun is up.\nGives one the shivers it does!	鼠人！他们是最糟糕的生物！他们日夜不停地在我们的垃圾堆里面乱挖！老是在黑暗中鬼鬼祟祟地乱跑！太阳升起之后他们就通通不见了。真是让我浑身起鸡皮疙瘩！\nGives one the shivers it does!
The Cyclopes that dwell to the southeast of here have always been a threat to the village of Rust!\nBut the village has always been able to push back any raid.\nThey aren't so tough in small numbers, but if they can catch you out in the desert unaware, you will not survive!	居住在这里东南方的独眼巨人一直都是铁锈镇最大的威胁！但是，这座城镇原先可以抵挡任何种类的攻击。他们数量不多的时候没什么好担心的，但是，如果他们在沙漠中出其不意地突袭你，你就很难逃过一劫！
The sea of fire took us all by surprise.\nIt spread through the eastern half of the village like wildfire.\nLuckily its growth seems to have slowed.\nStill I don't know how much longer any of us can last.	火焰之湖是个让我们意料外的状况。它就像是野火一般地在村庄的东半边扩散。我们很幸运，因为它的扩散已经减缓下来了。但是我依然很怀疑我们到底还可以撑多久。\nStill I don’t know how much longer any of us can last.
We need to find a new place to live!\nThere is no way any people could live under these conditions.\nEven the Cyclopes will be forced to move.\nThere are legends that talk about the ancient homeland that we traveled here from.\nThe stories say that it was lush and green.\nIf we only had such a place to return to.	我们必须要找个新的居住地才行！没有任何的生物可以在这样的环境底下生存。即使是独眼巨人都准备要搬迁了。谣传我们过去是从别的地方搬迁到这里来的。传说中也提到了该处是个丰饶、翠绿的地方。真希望真的有这个地方存在。\nIf we only had such a place to return to.
There are traders in Alvar and Shadowspire that trade for the different types of ore that can be found all over Jadame.\nThey use the ore to create items that they are more than happy to trade for more ore!	阿尔瓦和阴影塔地区的商人愿意贩卖贾丹姆大陆上所有你能够想到的矿石。他们可以利用矿石打造更多的物品，也因此愿意用这些物品换取更多的矿石。\nThey use the ore to create items that they are more than happy to trade for more ore!
You know, it is said the Vault of Time was here before Ravenshore; the town was built around it. It is supposed to contain the treasure of the old Emperor Thorn who ruled the lands around here hundreds of years ago. Many come here to try and open the vault, but none succeed.	你知道吗？据说时光金库在渡鸦海滨创建之前就存在了，这座城镇根本就是绕着该金库所创建的。金库里面应该藏着索恩帝王的财富。许多人来到这个地方想要开启该金库，但是都失败了。
As if Quixote was not enough of a problem to plague us, we must also contend with Ilsingore, Ilsingore and Old Loeb--renegades among our kind! They think they can rebuild our race by scattering across Jadame and starting their own tribes. Fools! All they do is weaken us by removing themselves from our numbers. Yaardrake has fled to the Ironsand Desert. I know not where the others have gone.	光是吉柯德就够让我们伤脑筋了。现在竟然又出了艾欣格和老罗伯这两个叛徒！他们认为可以靠着将部族四处扩散而重建我们的种族，并且聚集自己的部落。愚蠢！他们的所作所为只不过是让我们的数量更为分散，让我们的力量更为减弱而已。雅达克则是逃到了铁沙漠去，除了他们三个之外，我不知道还有其它飞龙离开了这个地方。
While Garrote Gorge has the highest concentration of dragons in Jadame, I hear there is hunting to be had elsewhere. For instance, there is rumored to be a great old dragon out in the Ironsand Desert. Another is supposed to be somewhere in the badlands of Ravage Roaming.	虽然绞刑峡谷地区是飞龙密度最高的地方，但是据说其它的地方也在进行飞龙的狩猎。举例来说，谣传铁沙漠中有一只巨大的古老飞龙。其它的飞龙似乎躲藏在残破镇的恶地中。
When Charles Quixote brought us over from Erathia, we sailed past Regna Island. Since this was our first sight of Jadamean land on the voyage, it was an omen of our hunting expedition's future success that I saw an ancient dragon winging its way towards the island. How I wish I could get a spear in that one! It was the biggest dragon I'd ever seen!	当查尔斯·吉柯德带着我们从埃拉西亚渡海而来的时候，我们曾经航过雷格那岛。当我们第一次看到贾丹姆大陆的土地时，我发现了一个好兆头：一只巨大无比的飞龙朝向该处飞去。我真希望可以狠狠地刺上它一矛！那是我所看过最大的飞龙！
Yes, I was here the day the Destroyer walked through Ravenshore! I was at the blacksmith's getting a knife sharpened. He walked right past us like he owned the town. A crowd was following him at a distance. One of them threw a rock, but it bounced off a magic shield surrounding him. He didn't even seem to notice. Later I heard an explosion from the town square. I ran out to look, but the Destroyer was gone and that crystal and scattered bodies were all that remained.	没错，当毁灭者走进渡鸦海滨的时候，我就在现场！我当时正在打铁铺请铁匠磨利我的小刀。他走过人群之中，彷佛君临天下。有群人远远地跟踪着他。其中一个人丢出一块石头，但它彷佛击中魔法防护罩一样地弹开了。他似乎根本没注意到。稍后，我听到城镇广场传来一阵爆炸。我跑去看，但毁灭者已经离开了，该处只剩下水晶和尸体。
Yeah, I saw the "Destroyer" and well named he is! I thought he was a rich merchant, and he looked like easy prey, so I…well, I tried to rob him. I ran ahead of him and lay in ambush in an alley, waiting for him to pass. When he walked by, I made a grab for him. My mistake! I didn't even touch him! As soon as I got near him, I was thrown back by his magic. In fact, I was thrown over a house into the next street!	没错，我看见了“毁灭者”，这还真是个不错的名字！我以为他是名富有的商人，看起来是个相当容易撂倒的家伙，因此我……我试着抢走他的东西。我跑到他前头，躲在巷子里面想要偷袭他。当他走过去的时候，我扑上去！但这真是一个大错误，我甚至连碰都没有碰到他！我一靠近，就立刻被他的魔法给弹了开来。事实上，我被弹过一座屋子，飞到隔壁的街上去了！
Those cursed ratmen--those so-called "Priests of Eep"--are nothing but trouble. More like a pack of thieves than holy men, they are! I'll warn you, traveler. Keep an eye on your equipment when they're around. They'll steal anything that isn't nailed down.	那个该死的鼠人，也就是那些什么伊普的牧师，只会制造麻烦而已。他们不像神职人员，只是一群小偷而已！我警告你们，当他们靠近的时候，千万小心自己身上的财物。他们会偷走任何没有钉在地上的东西。
Well, if you're looking for cheese, I daresay you will find it in the churches of Eep. You know mice like cheese. Well, this rule also applies to the ratmen of Eep. It is said that they keep a horde of the stuff in each of their temples.	如果你想要寻找奶酪，我建议你去伊普教堂找找看。你知道的，老鼠喜欢奶酪。鼠人也是一样。据说他们在每座神庙里面都有一大堆的奶酪。
On top of all of our problems here, the lord of our plane, Gralkor the Cruel is missing. He is a being of great power and could no doubt fight the dark magic which assails this plane.	除了我们所面对的一大堆问题之外，统治这个领域的元素之王也跟着失踪了。他是拥有强大力量的生物，一定可以抵抗驱使我们的这种黑暗的魔法。
I wish I understood what has happened here. One day it was life as usual here…and this is normally a peaceful realm. Then suddenly everyone was howling with violent madness and was filled with a thirst to crush your land of Jadame. Even now my fellow Earth Plane denizens work single-mindedly to push the firmament of our realm into yours.	我希望我自己了解到底发生了什么事情。那原本是和平常一样宁静的一天，这个空间原本十分的祥和。突然之间每个人都开始疯狂地嚎叫，胸中充满了欲望，想要破坏你们的贾丹姆大陆。即使是现在，我的土元素同胞们也在齐心一致地将这个空间推进你们的世界中。
Perhaps the reason I am not affected by the madness is that I carry with me an amulet given to me by the lord of this plane, Shalwend. It is said in our legends that he and the other elemental lords made your world. No doubt if Shalwend was here he could stop the creeping madness, but he is missing! Where is our lord in our time of greatest need?	也许我没有被这种疯病影响的原因是我身上带着有此地的元素之王所送给我的护身符。据说是他和其它的元素之王联手创造了你们的世界。如果他在这边，他一定可以阻止这场疯狂的暴乱，但他不见了！当我们最需要它的时候，元素之王怎么会不见了呢！
It was clearly magic which caused the madness that has taken my fellow Plane of Air dwellers. On the day it struck there was instant chaos. All who were affected dropped what they were doing and began to howl. It was a chorus of horror! Only a handful of us maintain our sanity. Now, day by day, our glorious realm falls further and further towards a state of hellish decline.	很明显的是魔法的力量让我们这个风元素界陷入了完全的疯狂中。当这个疯病出现的时候，一切立刻陷入了混乱之中。所有受到影响的生物都放下手边的事情，开始狂乱地嚎叫。这真是一场恐怖的暴动！我们之中只有极少数还保持了清醒的神智。一天又一天，我们的世界慢慢地被这种混乱的力量给吞食。
I am not unschooled in the ways of magic. Clearly some spell of almost unimaginable power has affected those that live in this watery realm. My main curiosity is to know why this has occurred. Clearly no one would risk using such power if it was not to achieve some terrible end. All I know is that the Plane of Water makes war upon your realm. From here, I can discern no reason to it!	我对于魔法还算有相当的研究。很明显地拥有难以想像力量的魔法影响了水元素界。我自己非常想要知道到底为什么会发生这样的事情。很明显的，如果不是绝对必要，没有人会冒险使用这样的力量。我只知道水元素界目前正在和你们的世界作战。从那之后，我就完全找不出合理的解释了！
W-what has h-happened to my home w-waters? All are c-consumed by m-m-madness! And worse, our l-lord is m-missing! Where is our r-ruler, th-the mighty Acw-w-walander! W-without him w-we are d-doomed!	我—我的家—发—发生了什么事情？所—所有的人—都疯了！更糟糕的是，我—我们的统治者—失踪了！没—没有了他—我们就死定了！
I know to your eyes, this plane must seem a hell-scape. But I tell you, normally it is not so. Before the madness we lived here together in a state of peace and harmony. But then, one day the madness fell and our Lord Pyrannaste was nowhere to be found. Perhaps his absence caused the madness? It may be so, he has never before been gone from this realm in my memory.	我从你的眼中就可以看出来，这个地方糟糕透了。但是我必须说实话，这里本来不是这样的。在那股疯狂力量来袭之前，我们本来都生活在和平与祥和的状况下。但是，突然有一天，我们的大王消失了，疯狂的力量也跟着来袭。也许他的消失带来了这个疯病？也许是这样的，因为我记忆中他似乎从来没有离开过这个地方。
Though the madness affects almost all of those that live here, it has not robbed them of their cunning. As I am sure you are aware, they make war on your realm, but it is not with crazed abandon that they do so. Leaders have emerged among the affected, and these make shrewd military designs against you. Even now they mass near the gateway to Jadame in preparation for a massive assault.	虽然此地的大部分生物都受到疯狂力量的影响，但是他们依旧是相当聪明的敌人。我也知道他们目前正在你们的世界上征战。而且，他们并非是在疯狂中设计这一切。在那群疯狂的生物中，拥有最强战术头脑的生物规划了一切的战术。他们现在就正聚集在传送门边，准备一举攻下你们的世界。
I wish I could join with you. Sadly, our war with the Temple of the Sun takes up all of our time. The guild cannot spare me now.	真希望我可以加入你们，但是和太阳神庙的战争占据了我们全部的时间，也许等到这一切结束吧！
Well, now that things seem to be settled with our little war with the Temple of the Sun. Sandro has said that guild members may turn their attention to other matters. Since arriving in Jadame, I have seen little of it save for the lands of Shadowspire. Say...perhaps you could use my services!	现在，在我们和太阳神庙之间的争执已经似乎结束的时候，山德罗说死灵法师公会的成员可以把注意力转移到其它地方去。从我到达贾丹姆大陆之后，我就没有看过除了阴影塔之外的地区。也许……你们会需要我的帮忙？
I wish I could. Unfortunately, we make holy war with the foul Necromancers' Guild. All of the Sun Temple's resources, including myself, are dedicated to that cause.	真希望我可以。很不幸的，我们正在和邪恶的死灵法师公会宣战。所有太阳神庙的资源，包括我自己，都全心投入在其上。
Yes! I would love to join with you. Now that our holy war against the foul Necromancers' Guild seems to be going well, I find I have little to do around here. I will go with you if you will take me.	是的！我很愿意加入你们。因为现在我们对抗死灵法师公会的圣战是如此地顺利，我想我在这边没有什么事情可以作了。如果你愿意接受我的话，我们就可以一起去冒险。
Go with you? I think not. Why would I want to join up with a bunch of amateurs when I have all the action I can handle here? Don't make me laugh. What can you offer me that a life of Dragon hunting does not?	和你们走？我可不愿意。我为什么要和一群菜鸟一起旅行？别开玩笑了。你能够提供给我什么猎龙的生涯所不能够提供的呢？
Adventure with you? Yes I will. In fact, before he left, Charles Quixote suggested just that. "Nelix," he said, "if those alliance heroes come by, you ought to consider going with them." Apparently, he thinks highly of you. Well, his recommendation is enough for me. Shall we go?	和你冒险？我当然愿意。事实上，在查尔斯·吉柯德离开前，他刚好还跟我说过这件事情。“如果联盟的英雄出现在你面前，你应该要考虑和他们一起走。”很明显地，他对你们的评价很高。对我来说，有他的评价就够了。我们可以走了吗？
Go with you? I like the idea, but I must stay here. I am one of the last veteran warriors left here. I must stay to protect the village.	和你们一起离开？我很想要，但是我必须留在这里，因为我是这里唯一的老经验战士了。我必须要留在这里保护大家。
Yes, that sounds good. Though the village could use me to protect it, I think the best I could do for it would be to go with you. My people will be destroyed if the cataclysm is not ended.
