I myself do not have one so must stay indoors during the day.	我们吸血鬼一般来说不能够在白天出没，但是死灵法师公会的谭特创造了一个法宝来处理这个状况。吸血鬼只要配戴着龙葵护身符，就可以自由自在地在白天行走。很遗憾的，护身符的数量并不多。我自己就没有，因此在白天必须要躲在屋内才行。
Normally, we vampires need one of Thant's Nightshade Amulets to travel freely during the day, but since you've brought us the Nightshade Brazier, glorious night covers Shadowspire around the clock! Now those of us who don't have the amulets can walk about during the day...well, at least as long as we stay in the area.	在正常状况下，我们这些吸血鬼的确需要谭特的龙葵护身符才能够在白天行动，但是在你带回了龙葵灯炉之后，夜晚从此笼罩了阴影塔地区。现在我们不需要配戴护身符，只要待在这个区域里面就可以自由行动了。
Though it isn't my style, I must say a lot of folks around here like to cover themselves with all sorts of jewelry. They say it's fashion, but I just find it gaudy. Anyway, if you have any to sell go find Pavel in town. He does a brisk trade in all sorts of tacky junk.	虽然这不是我的风格，但是附近的人都很喜欢配戴各种各样的珠宝。他们说这是流行，但我觉得这太过俗气。如果你有什么珠宝要卖，请找此地的帕佛谈谈。他是这里负责处理这些怪异商品的家伙。
You looking for sunfish? Sorry, can't help you. Funny thing is, I had a whole catch of them just last week, but Pavel the merchant bought the whole lot. Maybe you can get them from him.	你在找翻车鱼吗？抱歉，我帮不上忙。还真是不巧，我上个礼拜还有一大箱的翻车鱼，但是帕佛刚好把它都买走了。也许你可以从他那边买到一些。
We have a small warehouse downstairs. You are free to use the chests there to store extra equipment. Put anything in there you don't want to wander off!	我们楼下有个小仓库。你们可以自由利用该处的箱子来储藏多馀的装备。如果你不想要带着东西到处乱跑，就储藏在这里吧！
I see a vision of you in which you take a letter from Elgar Fellmoon in Ravenshore to Arion Hunter, the leader of the Ravenshore smugglers. After this, you return to Fellmoon. Welcome to Emerald Island!\nI'll be your docent for your stay on the island.\nIt is my duty to see that you understand the rules of both the island and the contest.\nFor the duration of your stay on Emerald Island please refrain from gross violations of civilized behavior.	我敬佩你们的决心和勇气。只要比赛还没有决出优胜者，你们就还有机会去赢得胜利。\nFor the duration of your stay on Emerald Island please refrain from gross violations of civilized behavior.
Emerald Island is southeast of Erathia, and far away from pirates and other ruffians.\nIt would be ideal, if not for the constant swarms of dragonflies that infest the island.\nFortunately, the large number of armed and skilled people this contest has attracted should thin their numbers a bit.Lord Markham has supplemented the normally sparse village with a weapon, armor, and alchemist shop for your convenience during the contest.\nA temple and basic magical guilds are also available.\nI would suggest making sure that you are completely ready before you start off on the contest.	欢迎来到翡翠岛！我是你们在这个岛上的向导。我会向你们介绍这里的风土人情和本次竞赛的详细内容。你们在岛上最好安分守己点，免得发生不愉快的事。
The rules of the contest are simple:\nThe first person to return with the items is the winner.\nAll you need to do is collect a wealthy hat, a red potion, a longbow, a seashell, a musical instrument, and a floor tile from the Temple of the Moon and bring them to the judge to win.	比赛规则很简单。谁能第一个把所有物品找到并交给裁判托马斯，就是胜利者。参赛者要找到一顶漂亮帽子，一瓶红色药剂，一张长弓，一个贝壳，一件乐器，还有月亮神庙里的一块地砖。
Are you contestants in Lord Markham's Scavenger Hunt?\nHow neat!\nI'm here to provide entertainment to Lord Markham's entourage, the contestants, and to anyone else that would like to hear a song.	你们就是参加马克汉姆爵士寻宝竞赛的参赛者吗？多神气啊！我是唱歌的。我唱给马克汉姆爵士的侍卫们听，唱给你们这些参赛者听，唱给所有爱听我的歌的人听，为他们带来轻松和快乐。

Priests.\n[Falk gazes coldly at you] Well it is that you have come to see the wisdom of the Path of the Dark.\nNever regret your decision, and never look back.\nSouth of Bracada is an island called Evenmorn, and upon that island are the old temples of the Sun and the Moon.\nBoth are inhabited by the remnants of the two religions, their once proud churches reduced to two old temples fighting for a small island.The Church of the Moon was the founding religion for our current faith, the Path of Darkness.\nI propose we help them out and tip the balance in favor of the Church of the Moon.\nUsing the map that I sent you to find, convince a sea captain to bring you to the island.\nThere, bring aid and comfort to the Church of the Moon by defiling the altar in the Church of the Sun.\nThis will so weaken the Sun cult that the Church of the Moon will be able to overcome them in battle.\nReturn to me when you have done this.	（福克冷冷地看着你们）你们想加入黑暗之路，领略我们深奥的魔法。不过我得事先提醒你们，一旦选择了黑暗之路，就得苦心修炼，永无退路。布拉卡达南面有个叫做埃弗蒙的小岛。岛上有两个古老的神庙。一个是太阳神庙，一个是月亮神庙。太阳教和月亮教本来是两个势力很大的教派，但由于他们两家势同水火，是天生的死对头，经过连年征战，大家两败俱伤，如今只剩下岛上的这两座神庙了，然而他们仍旧争斗不休。\n 月亮神庙是信仰我们黑暗之路的，我想派你们去助他们一臂之力。你们可以用在执行成为神甫任务时我交给你们的地图，租船下海，到达那个小岛。你们只要捣毁太阳神庙的神坛，他们就会一蹶不振，这样月亮神庙就可以轻而易举地对付他们了。如果你们顺利完成任务，就可以回我这儿来了。

Advancement as a Druid is simple.\nYou must visit the three ancient temples to nature we erected centuries ago and pray at their centers.\nThey are in Tatalia, Evenmorn Isle, and Avlee, and look like circles of stone with an altar of water in their centers.\nWhere exactly I will not say, but exploring the land about them is part of the process.\nWhen you have visited all three Circles, return to me to detail your experience.	要升级为高级德鲁伊其实挺简单的。你们必须在我们建立的三个古老神庙里祈祷，它们分别在泰塔利亚、埃弗蒙岛和埃里。你们不仅要在神庙中央被石圈围着的祭坛中央祈祷，还得在神庙周围走一走，视察一下有什么情况。只要你们把这些事都能一一完成，我会升级你们为高级德鲁伊。

Becoming a Warlock for some is a simple process, and for others it is nearly impossible.\nYou need to find a familiar suited to your personality.\nThe familiar will help to focus your magical abilities and sharpen your concentration.\nSometimes a single familiar can be shared, other times not.\nThe magical effects will not be noticed by anyone but a Great Druid--someone who is already attuned to nature.\nNow, let me see what kind of familiar you will need...[Tor picks up an elaborately carved gem and peers at you through it.\nHe frowns.]\nHmm.\nWell, the good news is that you all need the same familiar, and when you find it, you will be very powerful.\nThe bad news is that you need to find a dragon familiar.\nYou must find a dragon egg.\nBring them back here and I will hatch it, and cast the familiar spell.\nThe baby dragon will imprint to you all, and you to it.\nYou may be able to find an egg in a dragon cave in the Land of the Giants.\n'Ware the mother, though.	要成为邪术师说难也不难。只要你们具有这方面的天赋，就可以不费吹灰之力地学习我们的本领，让我看看你们有没有这方面的天赋。（他拿起一个玲珑剔透的水晶球仔细观察）啊，看来你们果然有这方面的天赋，不过在升级为邪术师以前必须完成一个任务，去和龙打交道。\n 你们必须找到一只龙蛋，把它带回我这里。我可以施展魔法马上把蛋孵化，幼龙诞生后会在你们身上留下印记，使你们拥有龙的力量。听说在巨人领地的龙穴里就有一个龙蛋，不过你们可得小心龙妈妈，她可是个很厉害的家伙。
Hmmmm.\n[Tor looks at you carefully]\nNope.\nCan't teach you.\nYou chose the Path of Light.\nI can smell it on you.\nI can sympathize with your choice, but I know that none of our spells or rituals can promote you once you have started down that path.\nSorry.	嗯（仔细端详着你们），你们怎么选择了光明之路，我都能闻出你们身上正义的气味了。一加入光明之路，就是和黑暗之路分道扬镳，我们邪术师的本领你们一招也别想学了，快滚吧。

We request your services for one task.\nA rare and valuable tapestry rests in Castle Gloaming, an antique from near the Silence stolen from the wizards some time ago.\nI would like you to get the tapestry for me, as my normal associates don't have access to Castle Gloaming.\nIn return, I'll make sure that the large band of renegade swordsmen and goblins near Harmondale don't accidentally stumble upon your quiet, little village.\nYou have one month; don't be late.	我们需要你们帮个小忙。法师们有一块珍贵的挂毯，是从寂静时代流传下来的一件文物。黄昏城堡派人偷走了这块挂毯。那个地方守卫非常森严，驻扎了大批大耳怪和邪恶剑手，我的手下根本无法靠近那里。看来只有你们能胜任这个任务，想办法潜入城堡，把那块挂毯夺回来。不过记住，你们只有一个月的时间，超过期限他们就会发现你们的哈蒙代尔城堡，并会立即发动，好了，别浪费时间了，快去快回吧。
I see.\nWell, it would appear that the band of renegades has indeed discovered your little town, and has begun looting.\nYou may find your bank account a little, well, pinched.\nConsider this a lesson in punctuality.\nGo now and have a pleasant, pleasant day.	现在一个月的期限已经过了，那帮匪徒终于发现了你们的小城堡，如今他们正在哈蒙代尔大肆抢劫屠杀，看来你们的全部家当都难保了。这次就当作一个教导你如何守时的教训吧。走吧，去享受这愉快的一天去吧。
Excellent!\nI see that you can be trusted to perform well when called upon.\nI'll keep that in mind the next time I need a job done.\nYour reward is nothing more than status quo-- things could be much worse than they already are.\nGo now, I have no more time for you.	好极了，看来你们是值得信赖的朋友，下次我们有事一定再会来找你们的。由于你们的努力，哈蒙代尔城堡终于安然无恙了，我想这是对你们的最高奖赏。
You don't have the tapestry yet, and the clock is ticking.\nIt would be prudent to keep to the task at hand rather than bothering me with your babble.	你们还没有拿到挂毯，不过没关系，还有的是时间。在你们完成任务以前可别再来找我了。
A few years back, while mining underneath Stone City, we came upon quite a shock.\nIt seems that the Warlocks from Nighon had burrowed under the sea to make a sneak attack upon the shores of Erathia.\nTheir careless tunneling has sunken the earth around here until now the areas around the old dwarven barrows and, of course, Stone City, rest far above the terrain.\nWe discovered a force of troglodytes in our mining areas, with a tunnel leading back to Nighon.\nIf you could help us by ridding the tunnels of the troglodytes, we could get back to mining.\nCan you help us?	几年前我们在石头城地下挖矿的时候发现了一桩大阴谋。尼根的邪术师竟挖掘了海底地道，想通过地道偷袭埃拉西亚的海岸。由于他们大肆挖掘，致使那里的地层下陷，现在石头城和原来的矮人族墓地比起那块地都高出许多了。在我们的采矿区有一条地道可以通往尼根，但现在那里被洞穴人霸占着。如果你们能帮助我们赶走洞穴人，我们就可以继续采矿了。请你们帮帮我们。
There are still troglodytes roaming the lower mine levels.\nPlease remove them!	洞穴人还在那个低洼的矿区里逍遥自在。快去把他们赶走吧！\nPlease remove them!
They're gone?\nRouted back into the connecting tunnels to Nighon!\nExcellent!\nWe can get back to mining immediately!\nThank you so much for your help; take this as a reward for your services.	他们逃走了，顺着那条地道滚回尼根去了！真是太好了，我们现在可以开工采矿了，真是太谢谢你们了，这是你们应得的报酬，请收下吧。\nThank you so much for your help; take this as a reward for your services.
I have recently gained information that the forces of Queen Catherine, in Erathia, have begun to build a force to attack Deyja.\nBecause of the difficult terrain, they have begun to breed and train griffins to assist in their attack.\nThese griffins are being raised in both Erathia and the Bracada Desert.\nI need you to kill off all the griffins in those two areas so that the Erathians will be forced to drastically delay their plans of attack.	我已经得到确切情报，埃拉西亚的凯瑟琳王后正调兵遣将想要进攻迪雅。但由于迪雅地势险要，他们正积极训练狮鹫以在战斗中助他们一臂之力。狮鹫只生活在埃拉西亚和布拉卡达的沙漠里。我需要你们去那两个地方把那些讨厌的狮鹫杀个干净，这样就可以大大推迟他们的进攻计划。
You've killed the griffins near Steadwick, but you haven't dealt with the griffins in the Bracada Desert yet.\nFinish them both off and return to me.	你们只消灭了埃拉西亚的狮鹫，布拉卡达沙漠里还有很多，不把它们全部消灭就别来见我。
Excellent!\nYou've done a splendid job.\nHere, take this as your reward and know you've earned the respect of Deyja for your bold success.	好极了，你们干得真不错。这些酬金你们收下吧，整个迪雅都会感激你们的。
You've killed the griffins near Spyre Town, but you haven't dealt with the griffins in Erathia yet.\nFinish them both off and return to me.	你们只消灭了布拉卡达沙漠里的狮鹫，埃拉西亚那里还有很多，不把它们全部消灭就别来见我。
You haven't killed off all the griffins in either Erathia or the Bracada Desert.\nIt is imperative that you finish this task to prevent their invasion.	埃拉西亚和布拉卡达沙漠里的狮鹫多着呢！你们一定要把它们消灭干净才能阻止埃拉西亚的进攻。\nIt is imperative that you finish this task to prevent their invasion.
My brother, Elron, was an avid Arcomage player.\nHave you played Arcomage yet?\nHorribly addicting game, if I do say so.\nIn any rate, you can only play if you have a deck, and decks are hard to come by… so I guess you probably haven't.\nIn any case, Elron wanted to go over some strategies to help him win more games.\nUnfortunately, he didn't want to be in town because he didn't want anyone else to spy on him learn his tricks so he went to the White Cliff Caves southeast a bit from town.Elron hasn't been back in almost a week.\nI'm certain something horrible has happened to him, but if he's alive I'd like to know that too.\nWould you do me the service of locating him?\nI don't have a lot of money to pay you back with, but I'd be greatly in your debt.	我兄弟埃尔隆是个出了名的牌鬼，玩牌简直如痴如醉，已经到了不要命的地步。这种游戏真是害死人啊！如果你们要玩牌的话，必须先找到一副纸牌，不过要找到这样一副牌也挺不容易的。我兄弟为了提高牌技，四处寻访拜师，最近有人在镇子东南面的悬崖洞穴见过他，天知道他去这么危险的地方干什么？\n 埃尔隆已经有一个星期没回来了，现在活不见人，死不见尸，我真为他担心。如果你们能帮我个忙，打听一下他的下落，我会重重酬谢你们的。
Still no sign of him?\nI understand.\nIf you do happen to find out what happened to him, please let me know.	还没有他的消息。我知道要找他的确很不容易，你们要是有他的消息，别忘了告诉我一声。
Dead?\nOh dear!\nThose are certainly his cards, though.\nI don't want the cards; you can have them-- that game has cost me enough now.\nOh, poor Elron!\nI have a little money you can keep for your help, and thank you for finding out what happened to Elron.	什么，他死了，哦，我的天啊！对，这是他随时携带的魔幻牌。这东西我不要了，我已经受够了，你们要就拿去吧，玩牌可千万不要上瘾啊，不然埃尔隆就是榜样！这里有点酬金，你们一定要收下，谢谢你们帮我打听到他的下落。
The School of Sorcery here in the Bracada Desert collects a number of odd magical paraphernalia.\nA few years ago an item of particular interest was stolen from the School by raiders from Deyja.\nThis item, the Seasons' Stole, was worn by Priests of the Sun during religious ceremonies at the turn of the seasons.\nAs a representative of the School of Sorcery, I'm authorized to reward you for its return.\nOur last information placed the stolen Stole in the Hall of the Pit; I would suggest checking there first.	布拉卡达沙漠里的魔法学院里有很多神奇的魔法物品。几年前这个学院遭到迪雅的袭击，他们抢走了一件珍贵的魔法披风，它叫四季披风。光明主教在四季交替时要举行隆重的仪式，必须穿着这件披风。我接受魔法学院的委托，希望你们能帮助我们找到这件披风。据说有人在地狱之厅见过它，你们可以去那里找找看。
Don't forget there is a reward for the Seasons' Stole if you are able to find it and return it to me.	如果你们能找到四季披风，我们会给你们一大笔酬金的。
Excellent!\nThis most certainly is the Seasons' Stole.\nHere is your reward; you've done both the School of Sorcery and myself a great service.	好极了，你们这么快就找到四季披风了。这些酬金你们收下吧。你们为我们魔法学院做了件大好事，我们会记住你们的。\nHere is your reward; you've done both the School of Sorcery and myself a great service.

The Grandmaster of Learning, William Smithson, instructs from his home on the eastern island of the Evenmorn Islands.	学习术宗师威廉姆·戴维斯住在埃弗蒙岛最东端的小岛上。
The Master of Light Magic, Helena Mornigstar, can be found in the cloud city of Celeste!	光明魔法大师晨星海伦娜住在塞莱斯特的云中城里。

The Master of Dark, Seth Darkenmore, can be found in The Pit beneath Deyja!	暗黑魔法大师塞斯·达克莫住在迪雅地下的地狱城里。
The Grand Master of Dark Magic can be found in the necromancer castle, Castle Gloaming.\nAt times Archibald has been know to take the journey to Clankers Lab.	暗黑魔法宗师阿基巴德住在黄昏城堡的死灵法师殿里。他还经常去科兰克的实验室。
So YOU'RE the ones that won Lord Markham's contest!\nHow do you like your winnings so far?\nHad enough winning?\nIf I were you, I'd give him a piece of my mind!\nHis mansion is in Tatalia, near the wharf.	你们就是马克汉姆爵士举办的寻宝竞赛的优胜者！你们赢得比赛有什么奖赏，光一个小小的哈蒙代尔城堡就把你们打发了。我要是你们，就一定会缠着他多要些好处。他就住在泰塔利亚的码头附近。
Watch your wallet while you're here, my friends--Steadwick abounds in thieves.\nAs a matter of fact, we are the world capital of thieves!\nIt's common knowledge that Bill Lasker, the Master Thief, lives in the sewers beneath Steadwick.	你们在这儿得小心看着你们的钱包。斯坦德威克到处是小偷，是有名的盗贼之都。盗贼大师比尔·拉斯克就住在斯坦德威克下面的下水道里。
Back when I was in the army, I was assigned to scout out the watchtowers in Deyja.\n<chuckling> It's no good trying to go through the front door, so I usually looked around for another way in.\nThose Necromancers are so cowardly, they always have an escape route.\nAnd a way out is also a way in...	我在退伍前，是负责侦察迪雅的哨塔的侦察兵。要从那个哨塔的正门硬闯进去，简直是送死。可我找到另一个出口，原来这些死灵法师也都是胆小鬼，他们还另有一个小门用来逃命。哈，这些我可方便了。
Glad you found the way in the back door.\nYou probably avoided a really nasty ambush that way.\nI hope my advice was useful!	你们找到另一个出口了，看来他们的伏击计划这下泡汤了。怎么样，听我的话没错吧。\nI hope my advice was useful!
Bill Lasker may be a criminal, but he has worked for the Crown from time to time.\nSpecial missions, official denials of involvement, spying…that sort of stuff.\nQuite a few unusual events can be tied to Bill, but none to the Crown.\nSeriously, don't you think the Queen's men could find and arrest one man, even if he was hiding in the sewer?	比尔·拉斯克虽然名声不好，可他确实为王室立了不少功劳。有些任务大动干戈也无济于事，但他盗贼大师一旦出马就手到擒来。埃拉西亚王室少不了他，怎么还会抓他呢！
So…what goes around comes around, eh?\nGood job with that Carmine traitor!	真是天遂人愿，这就是叛徒卡米尔应有的下场。
You want to know about that traitor, Carmine?\nWell, she used to work for the diplomatic corps, until she went soft and signed up with the goody two-shoes wizards.\nNow she walks the streets of Celeste freely, without a care in the world for the people she left behind.\nSomething bad will happen to her soon, mark my words!	你们要了解叛徒卡米尔的情况。她本来是我们的一个外交使节，可惜不知道哪一天她听信了光明法师的蛊惑，竟加入了他们一边。现在她正在塞莱斯特悠闲地散步呢！等着瞧吧，这个女人终有一天会倒大霉的。
Looking for "work"?\nYou can find the Master Assassin somewhere in the Pit.\nHe'll have something for you.\nThere's always someone or something that needs killing, that's for certain.	想找活干吗？杀手大师就住在迪雅，他会给你们些活干的。这世道该死的人多的是，你们干这行不愁没饭吃。
The dragon is fallen--and at your hands!\nYou are a hero!	你们竟然可以亲手屠龙，简直太伟大了。\nYou are a hero!
Until recently, our local dragon Wromthrax the Heartless has confined his depredations to the hills in the south--eating goats, farm animals, and stray dogs.\nBut he has developed a taste for elvish blood, and now hunts us as well.\nIf you should care to confront him, his cave is in the north, west of the Mercenary Guild in the mountains.\nHe isn't always there.\nBe careful--he is mighty.	本来这里的巨龙劳斯莱克只待在南面的小山上觅食小动物，从不骚扰周围的百姓。可是最近它狂性大发，吃了我们精灵族不少人。它的洞穴就在雇佣兵行会西面的山上，它有时会外出猎食，不在洞里。你们要对付它得千万小心，这条龙实在太厉害了。
What, you wanna be a crusader too?\nThis town is filling up with them, I swear!\nWell, there's a crazy man living in the back of a building near the center of town.\nPeople keep trying to sign up with him, and he keeps rejecting them.\nGood luck.	你们想成为十字军？这个镇子里可到处都是十字军。镇子中央一所房子的后面住着一个怪人，人们都想见他一面，可他谁也不见。但愿你们运气好，能够见到他。
Just a minute, let me check.\nMirror, mirror, on the wall… <the witch peers into her mirror for a few minutes, then sighs> It's still Alice Hargreaves.\nShe lives in Castle Gryphonheart.\nShe's quite a noble beauty, that girl is!\nTakes me back to my youth, this one does.	等一下，让我仔细看看。（巫师朝镜子里端详了好一会儿）啊，正是埃莉斯·哈格，她住在格芬哈特城堡。多漂亮的女孩啊！不过我年轻的时候也不比她差。
One of our finest, most upstanding citizens is William Setag, considered by some to be the most black hearted villain alive.\nAs a matter of fact, he's one of our best agents.\nWizards and their ilk complain because they've been on the receiving end of his schemes too many times-- but he's about the best there is in his profession.\nIf you're looking for employment, go see him.\nHis tower is on the north shore of Deyja at a rather inaccessible point.\nHe doesn't like visitors, but he should be glad to see more students turn up at his door.	威廉姆·塞塔格可是我们这里最能干的人，他使光明法师们吃尽了苦头，所以他们对他恨之入骨，其实他只是以牙还牙而已。\n 你们要拜他为师吗？为了清净，他住在迪雅北面海滩上一个非常隐蔽的地方。尽管他不喜欢不速之客，但有人来拜师他还是不会拒绝的。
If you're looking for a teacher to begin serious monk training, speak with Bartholomew Hume--you can find him in the village south of the town of Harmondale.	你们想拜师学艺，去巴斯洛缪·海墨尔那里碰碰运气吧。他住在哈蒙代尔南面的村子里。
There is a breed of Medusa infesting the Red Dwarf Mines that is immune to magic.\nThese monsters are a real terror!\nI can only thank the Gods that these creatures haven't escaped the mine.\nWhat would we do then, beat them to death with our staves?	在红矮人矿盘踞着一伙美杜莎女妖，魔法对她们无能为力，真是些可怕的家伙。谢天谢地她们就待在矿井里，还没有出来作恶。不然就凭我们根本对付不了她们。
The Baa cult is gone?\nWell that IS good news!\nEveryone is in your debt, whether they know it or not.	羊鸣教终于瓦解了。我们真是太感谢你们了，人民不会忘记你们的。
A few years back, a weird cult called the Temple of Baa moved into the area.\nAt first they seemed popular, what with their free offers of healing, but it soon became clear they were devoted to darkness.\nNo one goes up there very much, now, and it seems that whatever evil gods were backing them have withdrawn their support.\nBut they're up there still, lurking in the shadows.\nCreepy.	几年前一个名叫羊鸣教的邪教霸占了这个地方。起初由于他们积善行德，免费为老百姓救死扶伤，因此名声大震，广受欢迎。可是不久他们邪恶的本来面目被揭穿后就很少有人去那儿了，而且他们膜拜的邪神似乎也不再青睐他们，于是这个教派日渐衰弱。可是他们如今还盘踞在这里，等待东山再起的那一天。
Glad to hear the Baa cult has gone missing, instead of my sheep!\nGood job!	羊鸣教完蛋了，这下我的羊群该太平了，真是谢谢你们。
Lots of sheep have gone missing since the Baa cult moved in--and I mean lots.\nNo doubt they were taken to the Baa temple for sacrifice.\nI'm going to go broke if someone doesn't do something about those cultists!	自从羊鸣教到了这里，我养的羊就少了好多，一定是被他们偷去祭神了。要是再这样下去我就要破产了。\nI'm going to go broke if someone doesn't do something about those cultists!
Ssspies abound in places like this, my friendsss.\nIf you wish your letters and communiqués to remain sssecret, you should use a cipher when you write.\nThere is a book in the Castle library that will explain everything, my friendsss.	这个地方到处都是密探，要是你们不想让别人知道自己写些什么，就非得用密码不可。在这里城堡的图书馆里有本书能破译所有的密码。
Eight years ago, we sent a team of scholars to what is now the Red Dwarf Mine to study the strange creatures that would occasionally show up there.\nThe first thing we noticed was that Ooze ectoplasm makes an excellent potion catalyst.\nThe second was that ooze just can't be physically damaged--they split apart or squish, then run back together. If you want to kill one, you'll have to do it with magic.	八年前，据说红矮人矿那里经常有怪兽出没，于是我们派了一队学者去看个究竟。我们发现原来这东西叫粘土怪，它可是做药剂的好材料。兵器对它不起作用，无论刀砍斧劈，它都能马上还原。要对付它们，只有靠魔法。
If you head south past the Wyvern Plains, don't go knocking at the door of that huge fortress you'll see standing in the middle of the water.\nIt's the Titan's Stronghold, and they don't much like visitors.	你们要是走到南面的维文平原，千万不要去湖中那个堡垒，那里是泰坦巨人的营地，他们可不欢迎不速之客。
Arena fighting is available to anyone who wants to practice against any kind of opponent.\nYou can get to the arena by visiting a stable on any Sunday and asking them to take you there.	谁要是精力太旺盛需要发泄，去竞技场角斗倒不错。不过一定要在星期天去驿站，只有那天才有去那里的车。
Arena Champships are held on each Sunday.\nThey are open to all--but be careful!\nYour opponents are chosen by the Arena management, not you!	每个星期天竞技场都举行比武大会，你们可以上那里碰碰运气。不过你们的对手可都是些不好惹的家伙。
Every graveyard or ancestral burial place seems to have a haunted house nearby--and ours is no exception.\nThere is a mansion near one of the plateaus on the surface in the northwest.\nIt is most certainly haunted.	每片坟墓附近都有一幢鬼屋，我们这也一样。西北面就有一个，里面尽是讨厌的游魂。\nIt is most certainly haunted.
Fear the touch of the Ghost as you fear the loss of your life.\nIt inflicts terrible spiritual wounds and drains your youth before your eyes!	千万别惹那些游魂，它们会强大的灵魂魔法，还可以使你们迅速衰老。\nIt inflicts terrible spiritual wounds and drains your youth before your eyes!
A few wars ago, my great grandfather worked on an escape tunnel from the Elvish castle to the Tularean Caves.\nI don't think it's ever been used--no attack on the castle has ever brought it down--but it sure is an interesting piece of history, don't you think?	很久以前我的祖父曾被招募去挖一条准备撤退时用的地道，这条地道从精灵城堡一直通往图拉利昂洞穴。由于城堡从未被攻占，所以这条地道就从来没有人用过，不过这也应该算是历史上一个伟大的工程了。
They say that elves are faeries, but in truth we are only distantly related.\nThe true faeries don't live in civilization, and they're just like the stories say--playful, cruel, magical, and dangerous.\nBut if you really want to meet them, you could visit them in their Hall under the Hill, as they call it.\nIt is just outside of the village, to the North and West.\nIt will be challenging to get to the front door...	人们总以为精灵就是神仙，其实两者差了十万八千里。其实真正的神仙可不会和人类混在一起。他们生性贪玩、铁石心肠、法术高强、脾气暴躁，如果你们真的想要见见他们，可以去小山下面的大厅。那座山就在村子的西北面，不过你们要从正门进去可得花点功夫。
I'm impressed--very few actually make it inside the fairy mound, or get very far.	你们竟然成功的进入了神仙冈，以前可从没有人敢这样做，真是太了不起了。
The forest is much safer now, thanks you to!\nThe oldest tree is very happy with you, and has included you in its history recitals.	森林现在重新恢复了宁静，这要感谢你们所做的一切。老树对你们非常赞赏，还把你们的事迹编成歌谣传唱。
A while ago, I saw some men come to the Tularean Forest at night with axes and torches, following some sort of map.\nWhen they left, they were laughing and happy.\nIt looked like they were carrying a big green rock.\nMy brother says they were a band of mercenaries from Tatalia.\nAnyway, the next day was when the forest went mad.\nIt's too dangerous to go there now.	从前我看见有人按照地图的指引，明火执仗地闯入图拉利昂森林，心满意足地拉走了一块绿色的大岩石，有人说他们是泰塔利亚派来的雇佣兵。第二天可不得了，整个森林都发怒了，它们疯狂袭击胆敢进入森林的任何人，如今没人敢去那里。
The Tularean Forest is so old that it has developed a mind…sort of.\nThe forest is vaguely aware of who walks through it, but only one part of the forest can communicate with people.\nThat's the oldest tree.\nIf you want to have a very long and slow conversation, it will be happy to oblige.\nLook for it on an island to the south east of the forest.\nIt's larger than the rest of the trees.\nYou can't miss it.	图拉利昂森林里的老树年深日久，都快变成树精了。它们能够发觉有人进入了森林，但只有其中年纪最大的一棵树会和人说话。你们要是想知道更多一些情况，可以去那里找它。它在森林东南面的岛上，比别的树高大得多，很好认的。
Anyone needing to earn some spare cash can visit a town hall in any major city to pick up a bounty hunting assignment.\nCity councils are always fearful of the latest man eating monster or bandit or mad wizard, and set bounties on the creature that is bothering them most this month.\nThere's no rhyme or reason to their choices, so don't try to figure it out.\nThey often pay quite well.	要是你们想挣点钱花，可以去各个大城市的市政厅接受赏金任务。这些市政厅每个月都会悬赏消灭一种生物，比如吃人的怪兽、邪恶的魔法师或是猖狂的歹徒，反正他们想干掉谁就会出钱招募杀手。重赏之下必有勇夫，你们也可以去试试运气。
If you ever get tired of amateur bounty hunts, and want to advance your career, speak with Ebednezer Sower in Avlee.\nHe only teaches followers of the shadow path, though.	要是你们厌倦了简单的赏金任务，想要把自己的本领更提高一步，可以去埃里找埃伯登泽·索渥。不过他只向选择黑暗之路的人传授本领。
Pirates been operating out of the tidewater caverns for twenty years.\nThey're not Regnans, and they aren't part of Bill Lasker's men, but they're still pretty mean.\nYou want to get in there, just get yourself out to the islands to the west.	海盗在潮水洞穴为非作歹已经有二十年了。这些年来他们无恶不作，犯下了滔天罪行。你们要是想找他们去算帐，可以到西面的岛上去。
Oh, so the map is found?\nWhat will you do with your newfound treasure?\nShare it, I hope!	你们找到地图了。这下找那些宝藏就容易多了。
Evenmorn Island isn't lost, so much as hard to get to.\nIt is surrounded by treacherous reefs, sandbars, whirlpools, and sharks.\nIf it's dangerous, the Island is surrounded by it.\nThere are only one or two safe ways in, and the map for those ways was stolen by pirates during the chaos of the recent wars.\nThey probably still have the map in their caves.	埃弗蒙岛可是个凶险的地方，它周围到处是旋涡和暗礁，不熟悉地形根本无法上岛。但是海盗们趁火打劫，趁着战乱偷走了埃弗蒙岛的地图，现在地图一定在他们盘踞的洞穴里。
Now that you have the map, I guess you'll be going there soon.\nBe careful--they say the island is cursed!	你们找到地图了，这下去那个地方就容易多了。不过你们还得处处小心，听说那可是一个被魔法笼罩的地方。
The boat captains would be happy to take any paying fare to Evenmorn island, but they don't have proper maps.\nThere's no way anyone is going to risk their ship in the reefs around the Island unless they have the map!	只要你们有钱，总有船会带你们去埃弗蒙岛。不过没有这个岛的地图，恐怕没人敢陪你们去送死。你们还是先去找地图吧。
If you don't mind the swamp and fog, Evenmorn Island is a great place to live.\nOf course, the Curse doesn't help--ghosts and skeletons roam the island freely--but other than that, the neighbors keep to themselves.\nThe Churches of the Sun and the Moon don't even fight each other very much anymore.\n<Heh!> probably means they're running out of adherents...	如果你们不介意泥沼遍地，终日迷雾遮天的话，埃弗蒙岛倒是个景色秀丽的地方。听说岛上到处是游魂和骷髅，但也没干过什么坏事。岛上的月亮教和太阳教也不象前些年那样打得不可开交了，看来他们都已经日渐衰败，成不了什么气候了。
Well, I don't have any golem parts--I never took that class--but there are students all over Bracada who have spare golem parts lying around.\nThey aren't worth anything unless you have them all, so they'll probably just give them to you for free.	我身上可没有什么人偶的肢体，我对这玩艺儿不感兴趣。不过我听说在布拉卡达有些人身上带有人偶肢体的一部分，这个人偶只有拼合完整才有巨大的威力，所以我想他们会很乐意给你们的。
You see all those golems walking around Bracada?\nEvery single one of them was made by the School.\nNot bad for students, eh?\nThey don't have much to say, but they're tough guardians and can tell when a follower of Darkness is near.\nYou should see them go into action when one of those Necromancers or Warlocks wanders into our lands.\nZowwwwwieeee!	你们在布拉卡达看见那些四处瞎逛的人偶了吗？它们都是在魔法学院中修炼的法师的杰作，不赖吧。这些人偶虽然话不多，却非常“嫉恶如仇”。只要有死灵法师或是邪术师踏上我们的土地，人偶们就会奋起进攻。咿咿咿呀！
Divine intervention has been found and copied?\nA miracle!\nI never expected to see it in my lifetime!	啊，你们找到普渡众生魔法了，真是个奇迹。我以为自己活不到亲眼目睹魔法的这一天了。
Our astrologers have determined that the missing Divine Intervention text lies somewhere deep under the ground.\nUnder Deyja, to be precise.\nThey fear that the current incarnation of the text has fallen into the Breeding Pit--the Necromancer's version of our Proving Grounds.\nOh, what must we have done to offend the Gods so?\nWe will never retrieve the text from that forsaken place!	我们已经掌握确切消息，失落的普渡众生魔法就在迪雅的地下，确切点说，是在死灵法师们修炼魔法的再生之地里。愿神宽恕我们的罪过吧，看来我们要永远失去它了。
All the grandmasters are very pleased with you.\nYou have done our profession a great service!	现在我们所有的魔法宗师都非常赏识你们，你们为我们教派做了件大好事。\nYou have done our profession a great service!
The spell of Divine Intervention, the highest of the school of Light magic, has been missing for centuries.\nIt is a curse, we believe, for our lack of devotion and faith to the Light.\nWe struggle, and some go forth to find the spell, from time to time, but none have been successful.\nEach time they find it, fate cruelly snatches it from our hands.\nOur Grandmasters have been bereft of this final, great spell for as long as anyone alive can remember.	光明魔法中最神圣的魔法--- 普渡众生已经失踪了好几个世纪了。这大概是神对我们亵渎神灵，玷污光明教荣誉的惩罚。这些年来我们尽力赎罪，四处派人寻找这个魔法，但每次都功败垂成。我们光明教派的宗师曾经练成过这种魔法，它的威力真是旷古罕见。
The only people in the world who make soul jars are the Warlocks of Nighon.\nIt is a secret of their profession--which is strange, since they have absolutely no use for them.\nStill, we are forced to bargain for the jars in order to advance our craft.\nOf course, we have wrested the jars from them, from time to time...but then they raise their prices in revenge.\nIt must be our fate to suffer so.	天下只有尼根的邪术师会制造灵魂之坛，这是他们的独门绝技。可奇怪的是这项技术对他们来说并没有什么用处，而灵魂之坛是我们修炼魔法必需的圣物。这些年来我们巧取豪夺，讨价还价，从他们那里弄来不少坛子，而他们开的价也越来越高，唉，这样下去可怎么办啊！
Every Necromancer aspires to be a Lich one day.\nAll you need is a Soul Jar, knowledge of the Ritual, and the magical skill to survive the journey to the Land of the Dead and return.\nThe body withers while the soul lives on in a glorious state free from hunger, disease, and injury.\nOnly shallow and superficial people would mind what the Ritual does to one's appearance.\nTrue practitioners hardly notice.	每个死灵法师都想有朝一日成为巫妖。只要有了属于自己的灵魂之坛，经过永生仪式，就可抛弃自己的肉身，让灵魂自由自在地游荡，再也不用忍饥挨饿，再也不怕病魔侵袭，也不用担心受伤流血。人们总说永生仪式是多么的可怕，多么的惨无人道，可是为了灵魂的自由，还有什么不可以牺牲呢？
Since you've discovered the circles on your own they have worked their magic on you.\nI hope you will refrain from telling others of their whereabouts, as I have done for you.	你们成功的找到了石圈，得到了它赋予的魔力。不过我希望你们不要把石圈所在的位置说出去，那可是我们的秘密。
Three circles of stones were built by the Druids long ago.\nSince hearing of their locations ruins ascension attempts for would be Great Druids, I can only give you a general clue--All are in the open, unhidden and bare, and all are distant from settlements.\nTo tell you more would spoil your Ascension.	德鲁伊们很早以前就造了三个石圈，听说那是他们升职为高级德鲁伊时必须在那里举行仪式。石圈所在的地方非常偏僻，你们可得仔细搜寻啊！
Originally, there were four Circles of Stone, one for each Cardinal direction and season, but the Eastern Circle was destroyed somewhere in Nighon during the wars between the Churches of the Sun and the Moon.	原来有四个石圈，分别代表了东南西北四个方向和春夏秋冬四个季节。可在尼根的东方石圈在太阳教和月亮教的战争中毁于战火。
The graveyard does indeed hold the bones of the ancient King, but the hallowed land will curse any who disturb it.\nI don't envy you this task.\nEach grave you open will unleash its curse, and possibly some other trap.\nBest be sure you're opening the correct grave when you begin to dig.	国王的尸骨确实在这片墓地里，不过这个地方被赋予了魔力，任何擅入者将死无葬身之地。你们只可以挖掘真正埋有国王尸骨的坟墓，其它东西一样都不要碰。
Though the barrows were built to deter thieves, a way was needed to navigate them during burials and ceremonies.\nNear each of the gates you'll find a plaque.\nThese plaques are cryptic, but they will keep you from losing your way, as long as you use them.\nNo thief yet has realized their significance, so once they enter the barrows, they never leave.	这片墓地的道路犹如迷宫，是为了预防盗墓贼的。不过为了方便其他人能进出坟墓举行葬礼，这里每扇门旁边都有一个神秘的金属牌，它是指路用的，有了这个就不会在墓地里迷路了。很多盗贼不知道这个秘密，往往是有来无回。
We built a tunnel leading from our Thunderfist Mountain to the Land of the Giants during the War.\nIt was there we discovered the Kreegan, and more titans and dragons then you can imagine.\nI suppose one who's familiar is to be a dragon could find eggs in one of the dragon caves of that terrible land.\n'Ware the mother...	在战争期间我们修建了一条地道从桑达非斯通往巨人领地。在那里我们不仅发现了克里根族，还有很多巨人和龙。既然那里有很多龙穴，龙蛋肯定也不少，不过谁敢上那里去送死呢！
I've heard legends that there is a dragon in the Land of the Titans greater than any that have come before.\nLesser dragons and titans do battle on the slopes of the mountain in which its cave resides.\nThis dragon is so huge, and so mighty that the other dragons feed it for fear it will one day leave its cave and devour them all in its fury.\nThe dragons believe it is a god.\nIf that is so, imagine its treasure...	我听说在巨人领地有一条奇大无比的龙，附近的巨人和其它龙看见它就逃之夭夭。为了太平，它们还经常向这条巨龙进贡物品，甚至把它奉为神灵。看来这条龙的龙穴里一定有不少宝藏。
During the wars between the Temples of the Sun and Moon, three statuettes were stolen from the shrines in the Bracada Desert, Tatalia, and Avlee.\nI represent a group of Druids that want these shrines back to their original form-- it is critical for their worship.\nTo this end, I am offering a substantial reward for anyone that will find the statuettes and place them on the shrines.	在太阳教和月亮教的战争中，布拉卡达、泰塔利亚和埃里三个地方祭坛上膜拜的石像同时失踪了。我接受德鲁伊的委托悬赏寻找这些石像，这可是德鲁伊修炼至关重要的圣物。如果谁能找到石像并把它们放回祭坛，我会以重金酬谢。

Great work!\nThe Druids are so pleased, they threw in a little extra for your fine performance.\nTake this… you most certainly deserve it.	干得好极了，德鲁伊对你们非常感谢。这是你们应该得到的报酬，拿去吧。

Dwarves live under the ground of the Barrow Downs--a barren collection of ancient gravesites built by their race centuries ago to honor fallen kings and heroes.\nNot all of the barrows can be reached on the surface--many are magically connected to each other through a system of teleporters and traps that protect the graves from those who would rob them.\nIf you look closely at the walls you should see markings that might help you navigate.	矮人族住在拜尔德斯的地下。那里有很大一片墓地，安葬着千百年来矮人族的国王和英雄们。那里的坟墓机关密布，密如蛛网，许多坟墓都由远距离传送装置连接。但如果你们仔细观察，会发现墓地的墙上有指路的记号，有了它你们就不会迷路了。\nIf you look closely at the walls you should see markings that might help you navigate.

The city of Celeste has been home to the Light for centuries.\nIt was established by King Magnus II\nin 499 as the centerpiece to the Kingdom of Bracada, and as a beacon of hope to the rest of the world.	塞莱斯特城堡千百年来就是光明教派的根据地。它是由马格努斯二世国王在499 年建造的，现在已经成为布拉卡达的中心了。长久以来这座城市一直是人民心目中正义和希望的象征。
What you see on the surface in Deyja is only a small part of the true Kingdom of Death.\nThere is an underground city they call the Pit.\nIt can be reached from the surface only by way of the Hall of the Pit in Northern Deyja.	其实地面上的建筑只是迪雅城的很小一部分，大部分建筑在一个叫做地狱城的地下城里。迪雅北面有个叫地狱之厅的地方，从那里可以进入地狱城。
Celeste is the magical city built by the Wizards of Bracada centuries ago, and anchored half in the mountains, and half in mist high above Bracada.\nYou must use one of their teleporters to get there--you can find it near the center of Bracada.	千百年前布拉卡达的法师们建造了塞莱斯特这个城市。它一半建在山里，一半被布拉卡达的迷雾笼罩。要到那里去，你们只有依靠布拉卡达中心的那个远距离传送装置。
There was another murder, recently, that's been all the talk around town.\nAnother body--young woman, so I hear--was found near the sewers.\nDrained of blood, for the love of Light!\nIt's those thieves living' in the sewers I tell you!\nRats, they are.\nSomeone should go down there and put them out of business!	最近有发生一起杀人案。我们在下水道附近发现了一个姑娘的尸体，肯定是下水道里的那帮窃贼干的好事，该是收拾那帮家伙的时候了。
So it was a vampire that was killing all those people!\nI hear you're the ones to get rid of it, too!\nThanks!\nEveryone feels much safer now.	啊，原来是吸血鬼作怪，残害了这里不少百姓。感谢你们能够除掉这一祸害，现在这里该平安无事了。
During the War, the Warlocks had dealings with some strange creatures called the "Kreegan" in the mountains south of Avlee.\nIt's impossible to get there from the mainland, but the Warlocks dug a tunnel under the sea from their island that leads to the Kreegan's home.	战争期间，邪术师和埃里南部山区的克里根魔族曾经打过交道。这个地方一般是没有办法上去的。不过邪术师在海底修建了一条地道，从他们住的岛屿一直通往克里根人的老巢，这可是一条捷径啊！
We…overplayed our hand during the War, and failed to win any territory on the mainland.\nOur "allies", the Kreegan, turned out to be very untrustworthy.\nPeople from Enroth call them devils--now we know why.\nAt just the moment when we needed them most, they turned and ran, leaving us to face the wrath of the armies of Erathia and Avlee.\nNext time, we won't be so trusting.	我们在这场战争中费尽心机，如今竟然在大陆上毫无立足之地。这全是我们的“盟军”克里根人干的好事。他们背信弃义，在我们最需要他们的时候竟不发援兵帮助，我们面对埃拉西亚和埃里联手进攻，寡不敌众，最终功败垂成。唉，下次我们该吸取教训了。
There's been quite a bit of activity in Deyja, people are saying.\nIt seems that Archibald Ironfist, the leader of Deyja (and former King of Enroth) has left the Pit for an island off the Eastern shore of Avlee.\nA lot of Necromancers followed him there--seems there's some sort of split going on.	据说迪雅发生了大变故，有人看见迪雅的领袖、恩洛斯的前任国王阿基巴德·艾恩法斯特率领大批死灵法师离开了地狱城。
Rumors have been trickling out of Nighon that King Roland of Enroth may still be alive.\nSome Warlocks have said that they personally saw him when they met with the foul Kreegan.\nThe rumors also say that the Kreegan are keeping him in a cage and mistreating him horribly.	在尼根有人传说恩洛斯的国王罗兰德还活着。有些邪术师说他们在与克里根人见面的时候曾经看到过他，他被克里根人关在一个笼子里，受着非人的虐待。\nThe rumors also say that the Kreegan are keeping him in a cage and mistreating him horribly.
They say you are being credited with rescuing King Roland!\nThat's quite a feather in your cap, my lords!\nQUITE a feather!	听说是你们拯救了罗兰德国王，你们真是太了不起了，简直是埃拉西亚最伟大的人物。
A Soul Jar is what a Necromancer needs to complete the Ritual and become a Lich--an immortal undead monster that retains its mind and memories from life.\nIt's pretty obvious that this is not a desirable condition for most people, which is why the market for these artifacts isn't very good.\nNonetheless, Necromancers will do almost anything to get more--their profession's very existence depends on them.	死灵法师要升职为巫妖必须得到一个灵魂之坛，以进行永生仪式。灵魂之坛对他们来说可谓生死攸关。为了得到更多的灵魂之坛，他们会不择一切手段，甚至付出生命的代价。
Although the Warlocks are technically servants of the Dark, they usually aren't violent.\nThey will defend themselves if attacked, of course, but they won't start a fight unless you start it first.\nThey're quite reasonable, as a matter of fact, and they take their business seriously.\nI've found dealing with them to be quite pleasant.	邪术师由于信奉暗黑教派，一直被人看作无恶不作的杀人魔王。其实他们遵守人不犯我，我不犯人的信条，从不骚扰百姓。而且他们待人彬彬有礼，做事认真负责。我和他们打过交道，觉得他们并不象传说中那么可怕。
The underground complex Clanker has created is surprisingly well defended.\nThe front door will keep out all magical attacks, and the island makes it difficult for any sort of large force to land on the island.\nSo far, Clanker has kept to himself, and no one has seriously tried his defenses, but his best protection comes from a series of teleport blockers inside the complex.\nNo magical invasion force can teleport past the front door until those blockers are brought down first.	科兰克的实验室地形复杂，守卫森严。实验室的大门是用特殊材料制成，任何魔法都打不开它，而且实验室所处的岛屿地势狭小，大批部队无法登陆。实验室最著名的防御设施是反远距离传送防护盾，有了它任何魔法师都无法施展时空之门魔法攻占实验室。
So much for the mighty defenses.\nWord is the Necromancers, led by Archibald Ironfist, teleported straight into Clanker's Laboratory and took over.\nI'm told that treachery from within had something to do with it, though.	虽然科兰克的实验室警卫森严，但阿基巴德·艾恩法斯特率领死灵法师通过远距离传送装置，一举攻占了那个地方。看来肯定有内应，不然实验室不会这么轻易地被占领。
Looking for Expert instruction in one of your Skills?\nExpert teachers for most Skills can be found in Harmondale, Erathia and the Tularean Forest.	想要使自己的技术达到专家级水准。告诉你们吧，大多数技术的专家住在哈蒙代尔，埃拉西亚和图拉利昂森林。

The teleporters of central Bracada can get you to numerous locations in the desert.\nJust check the pillar in front of the teleporter for the location it will take you to.\nBe careful however, some of the teleporters haven't been working correctly and will not show the destination!	通过布拉卡达中央的远距离传送器，你们可以到达沙漠中的很多地方。你们可以仔细查看一下传送器旁边的柱子，上面写明了所要到的地方。不过你们得小心，有时候这个传送器会出点小问题，不知会把你们送到哪里。

Do not believe you have won!\nSoon the Iron Fist will lose its grip, and I will be your king!\nAnd you can be sure I won't forget you…	你赢啦？做梦吧！艾恩法斯特王朝快要完蛋了，我将成为王国的主宰。不过，你放心，我不会忘记你的。
As long as I am a member of this council, you will not be permitted to visit the Oracle.\nI am not convinced of your sincerity and will not grant your request.	只要我还在议会中有一席之地，我就不会赞让你拜访神使，我对你的忠诚度有所怀疑，不能满足你的要求。
You have not adequately demonstrated your loyalty to my liege, Lord Humphrey, and I cannot give you my vote.	你并没有发誓效忠我的主人汉弗里爵士，我不能对你投赞成票。
Followers of Baa and a band of cutpurses have been raiding Mist from the islands to the north.\nThey come to Mist through a teleporter on the northwest of the island.\nI'll activate the teleporter so that you can get to their islands and get rid of them.\nI'm sure they have all the things they've stolen on those islands, and you can get back from their teleporter.	一伙羊鸣教教徒和作恶多端的匪徒袭击并霸占了浓雾岛。他们是通过西北方岛上的一个远距离传送装置上岛的。我已经启动了远距离传送装置，你可以通过它上岛把它们一网打尽。我肯定它们偷来的赃物都藏在岛上。你可以通过它们的远距离传送装置返回。

Doom	末日审判
Road to Insanity	疯狂之路
Beyond Madness	超越疯狂
Austerity	苦行
No bosses. Monsters have half the health and deal half the damage of MAW.	无首领。怪物生命与伤害均为 MAW 的一半。
No bosses. A gentler start for players new to MAW.	无首领。为初次接触 MAW 的玩家提供更平缓的开局。
The intended experience. Every map has bosses with special abilities.	设计者的预期体验。每张地图都有具备特殊能力的首领。
Larger groups and better loot. Attacking while flying ends Fly on most maps.	更大的怪物群体与更好的掉落。在多数地图上，飞行中攻击会结束飞行状态。
As Hard; melee monsters outdoors can also gain a ranged attack.	同困难难度；野外近战怪物还可能获得远程攻击。
Saving costs food, and Lloyd's Beacon and Town Portal fail near enemies; dungeons left below 65 % cleared revive their dead.	存档消耗食物，罗德的信标与城镇传送门在敌人附近会失效；清理度低于 65% 的地下城将复活其中的怪物。
Cannot be lowered later.	之后无法下调。
Monsters on a new continent gain the levels you earned elsewhere. Suits playing one continent at a time. Masteries need skill 4, 7 and 10.	新大陆的怪物会获得你在其他大陆练就的等级。适合一次只玩一块大陆。宗师级需要技能等级 4、7、10。
Every map has a fixed level, about 2.5× its original band; travel between continents to progress. Suits clearing the whole game. Masteries need skill 6, 12 and 20.	每张地图等级固定，约为原版的 2.5 倍；需往返各大陆推进。适合打通整个游戏。宗师级需要技能等级 6、12、20。
MAW's rules with no extra challenge.	MAW 规则，无额外挑战。
Monster hits cause conditions, more and stronger monsters spawn, items must be identified before wearing and hirelings are limited. Masteries need skill 6, 12 and 20. Starts with 5,000 gold and bonus experience.	怪物攻击会附加异常状态，出现更多更强的怪物，装备须鉴定后才能穿戴，随从数量受限。宗师级需要技能等级 6、12、20。开局 5000 金币与额外经验。
Doom's monsters, no map refills, and masteries need skill 8, 16 and 25. Starts with 5,000 gold and bonus experience.	末日审判的怪物设定，地图不再刷新，宗师级需要技能等级 8、16、25。开局 5000 金币与额外经验。
Insanity, and the party restarts at level 1, maps reach level 500, bosses are seeded and every death counts, including loading during combat. No mercenaries. Horizontal progression; masteries need skill 10, 24 and 40.	在疯狂之路基础上，队伍从 1 级重新开始，地图最高 500 级，首领固定生成，每次死亡都会被记录——包括战斗中读档。无雇佣兵。横向成长曲线；宗师级需要技能等级 10、24、40。
No Legendary or Celestial items and no crafting rewards, weaker enchantments and reagents, flatter bosses and a minimum armour value from the armour skills. Combines with any difficulty.	无传说或神圣物品、无合成奖励，附魔与试剂更弱，首领更平滑，护甲技能提供最低护甲值。可与任意难度叠加。

Monsters keep pace with the party's level. Takes effect on entering an area.	怪物跟随队伍等级成长，进入新区域时生效。
Standard games only; this game's ruleset scales its monsters itself.	仅标准游戏可用；本局规则集会自行缩放怪物。
Impact sounds by what was struck, and a brief pause of the struck creature.	按受击材质播放打击音效，并使受击生物短暂顿挫。
Nudge the view along the blow on critical hits and kills.	重击与击杀时镜头随打击方向轻晃。
Skip event movies. Leave off for a first adventure.	跳过剧情影片。首次冒险建议保持关闭。
Show damage numbers and combat messages in the world.	在游戏世界中显示伤害数字与战斗信息。
A button beside the followers' head opens a log of recent hits, each with its damage steps.	队友头像旁的按钮可打开最近战斗记录，逐条显示伤害构成。
Describe each attack in the status bar.	在状态栏逐条描述每次攻击。
Over the target, recent combatants, or all nearby visible enemies.	显示于目标头顶、近期交战者，或所有附近可见敌人。
Show current and maximum health above the bars.	在生命条上方显示当前与最大生命值。
Briefly show recently lost health behind the current fill.	在当前血量之后短暂显示刚失去的生命。
Mark characters with quests to offer or complete, where the world has them.	在世界地图上标记可接取或交付任务的角色。
Offer actions for the object under the pointer.	针对指针所指的物体弹出可执行操作。
Draw the frame behind the party portraits during play. Other screens always keep it.	游戏进行中绘制队伍头像边框，其他界面始终保留。
Show the round potion quick-slot button at each portrait. The Potion Wheel key opens the ring either way.	在每个头像旁显示圆形速捷药剂按钮；无论开关，药剂转盘键均可打开圆环。
Outline items in their rarity colour (rulesets with rarities, such as MAW).	按稀有度颜色勾勒物品轮廓（限带稀有度的规则集，如 MAW）。
Look for a new version on jasicek.net when OpenYAMM starts. Nothing downloads without asking.	OpenYAMM 启动时到 jasicek.net 查找新版本，未经询问不会下载任何内容。
Changes must be confirmed within 15 seconds.	更改必须在 15 秒内确认。
Borderless uses your desktop resolution.	无边框模式使用桌面分辨率。
Prevent screen tearing by matching frames to the display.	让画面帧与显示器刷新对齐，消除画面撕裂。
Sets most options below at once; changing one of them shows Custom.	一键设定下方大多数选项；单独改动任何一项后会显示为「自定义」。
Smooth jagged edges and shimmering foliage.	平滑锯齿边缘与闪烁的 foliage。
Longer distances can reduce performance outdoors.	更远的视距可能降低户外场景性能。
Draw simpler creature shapes far away; nearby creatures keep full detail.	远处生物使用简化模型，近处保持完整细节。
Off keeps the sharp, pixelated texels of the original.	关闭时保留原版锐利的像素纹理。
Keep distant ground textures sharp (anisotropic filtering).	让远处地面纹理保持清晰（各向异性过滤）。
Animated shadows of creatures; higher quality adds detail and range.	生物的动态阴影；更高质量带来更多细节与更大范围。
How finely torches, spells and other lights are sorted; Low suits phones.	火炬、法术等光源的细分精度；低档适合手机。
Lights tint their surroundings with their own colour.	光源用自己的颜色为周围环境着色。
Torch Light glows firelight orange instead of the original white.	火炬发出暖橙色火光，而不是原版的白光。
Add soft shading where solid surfaces meet.	在实体表面交界处添加柔和阴影。
Draw extra ground decorations where available.	在有素材处绘制额外的地面装饰。
Density of falling rain and snow; Off hides them but the weather goes on.	雨雪的密度；关闭后不再显示，但天气仍在运作。
Rain darkens the ground and buildings, which dry slowly afterwards.	雨水打湿地面与建筑，随后缓慢晾干。
Smooth rock and wet ground catch the sun and nearby lights.	光滑岩石与湿地反射阳光和附近灯光。
Reflect the sky, ground and buildings in water. Costs extra rendering.	水面倒映天空、地面与建筑，需要额外渲染开销。
Higher detail gives sharper reflections at a higher cost.	更高细节带来更清晰的反射，开销也更大。
Include nearby creatures, trees and decorations.	把附近的生物、树木与装饰物也纳入反射。
Small ripples from creatures moving on water.	生物在水面移动时泛起细小涟漪。
Raindrop rings on still water while it rains.	下雨时静水上泛起雨滴圆环。
Flames, glowing crystals and spell light bloom softly into their surroundings.	火焰、发光水晶与法术光芒向四周柔和晕染。
Bring out shadow detail and soften over-saturated earth tones.	增强阴影细节，柔化过饱和的大地色调。
Sharpen the 3D view; the interface is unaffected. Zero turns it off.	锐化 3D 画面；界面不受影响。设为 0 关闭。
Show a slash, thrust or crush where melee attacks connect.	近战命中处显示劈砍、突刺或重击特效。
Briefly brighten a creature struck in melee.	被近战击中的生物短暂发亮。
Add blood effects when melee attacks connect.	近战命中时添加血液效果。
Leave a pool of blood beneath slain creatures.	被击杀的生物身下留下一滩血。
Weapons, spells, the interface and the world. Zero mutes.	武器、法术、界面与世界音效。设为 0 静音。
Music in menus, towns and dungeons. Zero mutes.	菜单、城镇与地下城的音乐。设为 0 静音。
Spoken character reactions and dialogue. Zero mutes.	角色的语音反应与对白。设为 0 静音。
Play walking and running sounds.	播放行走与奔跑声。
Modern: WASD, left-click attack, E to use. Classic: arrow keys, A to attack, left-click to use.	现代：WASD 移动、左键攻击、E 互动。经典：方向键移动、A 攻击、左键互动。
How fast the view follows the mouse. Zero turns mouse look off.	视角跟随鼠标的速度。设为 0 关闭鼠标视角。
Base turning speed, from the original 16× and 32× to the faster Smooth.	基础转向速度：原版 16 倍、32 倍，或更快的「平滑」。
Move the mouse up to look down, and down to look up.	鼠标上移向下看，下移向上看。
Run by default instead of walking.	默认奔跑而不是步行。
Number keys on the inventory page and in shops and chests; Shift with a number does the other. A party of one opens bags.	数字键用于行囊页及商店与宝箱；Shift+数字执行另一侧。单人队伍直接打开背包。
Chosen at the start of the adventure. The rules of each part follow below.	在冒险开始时选择，各项细则见下方。
Monster health and damage, how many come, loot and bosses. Nightmare can never be lowered again.	怪物生命与伤害、数量、战利品与头目。噩梦难度无法再调低。
Free lets monsters grow with the levels you gain elsewhere; Horizontal fixes each map's level.	「自由」让怪物随你在别处获得的等级成长；「水平」固定每张地图的等级。
Chosen once at the start; fixed for the whole adventure.	开始时选择一次，整个冒险期间固定。
Your missiles follow their target.	你的飞行法术与箭矢会追踪目标。
Your attacks and spells can hurt peaceful townsfolk and guards.	你的攻击与法术可能伤及无辜镇民与守卫。
Drops you would rather not carry turn into gold. Opens the filter with its presets.	不想携带的掉落物自动换成金币。可通过筛选器配置预案。
A companion slain far past death leaves the party for the Adventurer's Inn.	队友倒下超过极限后离队，前往冒险者旅店。
